### docs/project-overview.md
# 项目说明

## 项目定位

TradingAgents-AShare 是一个面向 A 股投资者的多智能体投研系统，核心理念：

- **模拟真实投研机构**：14 个专业角色分工协作
- **多空辩论机制**：多头研究员 vs 空头研究员，研究总监裁决
- **多模型厂商支持**：OpenAI / Anthropic / Google / 智谱 / DeepSeek / 小米等
- **意图驱动交互**：自然语言输入，自动识别标的和周期
- **持仓追踪**：导入持仓，自动跟踪，连续分析

## 当前产品事实（2026-07-10）

项目已从单一报告生成器扩展为分层投研工作台，但产品主线应保持收敛：

```text
Tree Work 既有研报/半年报知识页（历史上游）
  -> ZCode 知识库接入、研究评分与快照发布
  -> 来源与事实审计
  -> 昊天左侧候选
  -> TradeFlow 少量候选池
  -> 轻量 TA / 人工确认的完整 TA
  -> 观察仓、盘中观察、盘后复盘
```

TradeFlow 的 `TECH_TRADE` 与 `POLICY_AMBUSH/POLICY_CONFIRM` 必须分池展示，不能把短线技术机会当作中长期昊天候选。Fast Radar、Light Research、Full TA 的运行层级也必须保持清晰：完整 TA 只能人工确认触发。

现有 Tree Work 知识页只作为可追溯的历史上游与背景/观点证据；后续知识库接入、`research-scorer` 和正式快照发布统一由 ZCode 负责。公告、原始财报和行情数据仍是事实与动作门禁的优先来源。所有知识命中都必须带来源路径、报告期或更新时间、来源等级和新鲜度。

## 核心架构

### 1. 分析师团队（7 个）

| 角色 | 职责 | 数据源 |
|------|------|--------|
| 基本面分析师 | 财务指标、估值分析 | Tushare Pro（结构化主源）/AKShare/BaoStock |
| 情绪分析师 | 社交媒体情绪、舆情 | AKShare |
| 新闻分析师 | 新闻事件、政策影响 | AKShare |
| 技术分析师 | K线形态、技术指标 | AKShare/BaoStock |
| 宏观分析师 | 宏观经济、利率环境 | AKShare |
| 主力资金分析师 | 大单流向、主力动向 | Tushare Pro（个股资金主源）/AKShare |
| 量价分析师 | 成交量、量价关系 | AKShare/BaoStock |

### 2. 研究员团队

- **多头研究员**：综合分析师结论，构建看多 Claim
- **空头研究员**：综合分析师结论，构建看空 Claim
- **研究总监**：裁决多空辩论，形成投资计划

### 3. 风控团队

- **风控经理**：评估风险、设置止损止盈
- **合规检查**：确保建议符合投资规范

### 4. 交易员

- 根据研究计划和风控要求，输出最终交易建议

### 5. 编排层

- **LangGraph**：状态图编排，控制 Agent 间信息流转
- **辩论机制**：多轮辩论，每轮有 Claim → Counter-Claim → 裁决

## 数据流

```
用户输入（自然语言）
  ↓
意图识别 → 标的 + 周期
  ↓
数据获取（7 路并行）
  ↓
分析师团队（7 个并行分析）
  ↓
研究员辩论（多轮）
  ↓
风控评估
  ↓
交易建议输出
  ↓
研报存储 + 通知推送
```

## 模型配置

系统支持多层模型配置：

| 配置项 | 说明 | 默认值 |
|--------|------|--------|
| `deep_think_llm` | 深度思考模型 | gpt-4o |
| `quick_think_llm` | 快速思考模型 | gpt-4o-mini |
| `mid_think_llm` | 中等思考模型 | gpt-4o |
| `ultra_think_llm` | 超深度思考模型 | gpt-4o |
| `backend_url` | API 端点 | https://api.openai.com/v1 |

支持通过环境变量或前端设置页切换模型厂商。

## API 接口

后端 FastAPI 提供以下主要接口：

- `POST /api/analyze` — 启动分析任务
- `GET /api/analysis/{id}` — 获取分析结果
- `GET /api/holdings` — 获取持仓
- `POST /api/watchlist` — 管理自选股
- `GET /api/reports` — 历史研报
- `POST /api/scheduler` — 定时任务管理

## 前端功能

- 意图驱动的自然语言交互
- 辩论对战可视化（多空对抗 Drawer）
- 持仓追踪与跟踪看板
- 结构化研报管理
- 定时分析配置
- 模型厂商设置
### docs/TASKS.md
# 任务池

> 最后更新：2026-08-16

---

## 0. 自动领取规则与当前队列

### 状态规则

- `ready`：允许 `scripts/auto_dev_loop.sh` 自动领取。
- `in_progress`：已有 OpenCode/OpenClaw/Codex 半成品或人工正在处理，自动开发不得重复领取。
- `blocked`：前置任务、人工确认、权限或成本条件未满足。
- `done`：已完成并有 commit / 验收记录。
- `proposed`：候选任务草案，只能人工或 Codex 确认后转为 `ready`。

### 自动领取顺序

1. 自动脚本只领取 `状态=ready` 的任务。
2. 同优先级按文档顺序领取；批量模式会在任务完成后继续领取下一个 `ready` 任务，直到无任务或某个任务失败。
3. 工作区不干净时必须停止，先进入人工/Codex review，不允许覆盖半成品。
4. 每个任务必须写入 `docs/task_runs/<TASK_ID>-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS/` 运行档案。
5. 通过任务必须同时更新 `docs/TASKS.md`、`docs/DEVLOG.md`。

- **描述**：把计算后的计划仓位传入加仓检查；第一次大跌/跌停未打开禁止进攻仓；试错仓补最大下行约束。
- **优先级**：P1
- **状态**：done — 130 项专项与 517 项相关回归通过；Codex review 无 correctness finding
- **depends_on**：F-001-R1
- **auto_release**：true

### SCORE-001-R1: 快照 loader 验收档案修正（P2）
- **描述**：修正文档中不可达/过期 commit 引用和 run archive 的 CLAIMED 状态，不改生产 loader。
- **优先级**：P2
### docs/DECISIONS.md
# 架构决策记录

> 记录项目中的关键架构决策，方便后续回溯"为什么这样做"。

---

## ADR-001: 采用 LangGraph 编排多智能体
- **日期**：项目初始化
- **背景**：需要 14 个 Agent 按特定流程协作，有循环（辩论）和条件分支
- **决策**：使用 LangGraph 状态图编排，而非简单的链式调用
- **原因**：LangGraph 原生支持循环、条件路由、状态管理，适合辩论机制
- **影响**：所有 Agent 间通信通过 Graph State 传递，新增 Agent 需要注册到图中

## ADR-002: 多模型厂商适配层
- **日期**：项目初始化
- **背景**：不同 Agent 对模型能力需求不同，用户可能有不同的模型偏好
- **决策**：通过 `llm_clients/` 统一适配，支持通过配置切换厂商和模型
- **原因**：避免硬编码单一厂商，降低用户使用门槛
- **影响**：新增模型厂商需要在 `llm_clients/` 中添加适配器

## ADR-003: SQLite 作为持久化方案
- **日期**：项目初始化
- **背景**：需要存储研报、自选股、定时任务等结构化数据
- **决策**：使用 SQLite，单文件部署，无需额外数据库服务
- **原因**：本地部署场景，轻量级，零运维
- **影响**：并发写入有限，定时任务和分析任务需要串行化写入

## ADR-004: OpenClaw 作为项目协作调度层
- **日期**：2026-05-09
- **背景**：需要 OpenClaw、OpenCode、OpenCode TUI 三者协作处理同一项目
- **决策**：OpenClaw 负责任务判断和调度，不直接做代码修改；OpenCode 负责代码执行；OpenCode TUI 负责审查
- **原因**：OpenClaw 有完整的 agent 调度能力（exec / sessions_spawn / cron），适合做编排层
- **影响**：所有代码修改任务必须经过 OpenClaw 判断复杂度后决定执行路径

## ADR-005: Git 仓库 + docs/ 作为项目事实源
- **日期**：2026-05-09
- **背景**：多个工具需要读取同一套项目上下文
- **决策**：不使用任何 agent 的独立记忆系统保存项目状态，所有关键信息写入 Git 仓库的 docs/ 目录
- **原因**：避免信息分散、版本冲突、单点失效
- **影响**：每次任务开始前必须读取 AGENTS.md + docs/ 下的上下文文件

## ADR-006: 2026-07-10 将研报/半年报/昊天闭环设为唯一产品主线
- **日期**：2026-07-10
- **背景**：系统已有 TradeFlow、跟踪看板、知识库和自动开发等大量工程能力，但真实候选中仍以技术池为主，观察仓和盘后 Review 的生产记录不足，半年报事实 Provider 尚未完成。
- **决策**：优先完成 HY-002/HY-003/KB-015 及其后续事实审计，再做昊天真实候选和交易日回放；PLAYBOOK-002~006 暂停，除非直接阻塞主线。
- **原因**：用户核心目标是从政策、产业和研报中找到少量高质量中长期候选，而不是继续扩大功能数量。先验证选股和证据闭环，才能判断后续仓位规则是否值得实现。
- **影响**：自动领取顺序以 `docs/TASKS.md` 的“当前执行队列”为准；fixture 验收和真实运行验收必须分开记录；新功能必须说明是否直接服务主线。

## ADR-007: 研究评分采用四卡分层所有权，不生成混合总分
- **日期**：2026-07-13
- **背景**：现有 Tree Work 知识库、TA readiness、TradeFlow 候选分、账户风控和 PLAYBOOK 已分别存在评分或阶段字段。若各层都计算完整总分和动作，会产生重复计分、阈值冲突，以及“好公司被误判为现在可以买”的风险。
- **决策**：`research-scorer` 的代码、规则版本和正式快照写入归 ZCode 知识库接入项目所有；ZCode 发布的知识快照只拥有慢变量 `research_evidence_confidence` 与 `thesis_quality`。现有 Tree Work 页面继续作为上游知识输入，不要求历史改名。TradingAgents-AShare 不复刻评分公式，只读取版本化快照；TradeFlow 复用现有动态因子生成 `entry_timing`，账户上下文生成 `portfolio_fit`，TA 在四卡之后统一执行硬性否决并映射 PLAYBOOK 七阶段。禁止合成单一股票总分，禁止知识快照携带或覆盖交易动作。
- **原因**：证据可信度、公司逻辑、实时买点和账户资格回答的是四个不同问题；分层后才能做到来源可追溯、动态变化可解释，并让高研究分始终受行情、账户和风控约束。
- **影响**：知识库侧任务单独记录在 `docs/zcode_research_scorer_handoff.md`；本仓库新增 SCORE-001~006 消费与裁决任务线。`execution_action` 与 Buy/Risk Level 继续作为底层安全契约，`playbook_stage` 只提供更细语义。SCORE-005 可以实现不含仓位数量的阶段判定，但不解锁 PLAYBOOK-002/003 的真实三笔法和仓位拆分规则。SCORE-006 必须等真实 ZCode 快照人工确认后释放，fixture 不得冒充跨项目验收。

---

## 决策模板（后续新增时复制）

```
## ADR-NNN: [决策标题]
- **日期**：YYYY-MM-DD
- **背景**：[为什么需要做这个决策]
- **决策**：[做了什么决定]
- **原因**：[为什么选这个方案]
- **影响**：[对项目的影响]
```

## 2026-08-16 | TA-TUSHARE-2000-001A-R1：权限矩阵补修与真实重跑（P0）

- 修复 c73ed4f Codex review（gpt-5.6-sol high）3×P1+2×P2：
  - **P1 Token 归属**：`scripts/audit_tushare_capability.py` 不再用
    `load_dotenv(override=False)`（继承环境变量可静默压过指定 `.env`）。新增
    `resolve_token()` 显式解析并记录来源：`.env` 恒优先于继承环境变量，返回
    `(token, dotenv_file/inherited_environment, env_override_applied)`；矩阵新增
    `token_source`/`token_env_override_applied` 字段（schema 1.0→1.1），Markdown、
    receipt 与 dry-run 输出均展示来源标识；新增 `--dotenv` 参数支持显式指定文件。
  - **P1 repurchase 全市场抓取**：`ENDPOINT_SPECS` 中 repurchase 由公告日窗口
    （scope=market_window_filtered，实测命中 2000 行上限）改为单 `ts_code` 最小查询
    （scope=symbol）。真实重跑验证上游接受 `ts_code`：2000 行→6 行（603629.SH，
    公告日 20240207~20260326），receipt 新增“repurchase 单股查询记录”章节。
  - **P1 泄漏扫描不阻断**：`write_outputs` 改为先组装 JSON/Markdown/receipt 全文，
    `scan_artifacts_for_secret()` 命中即**拒绝写盘**并由 `main` 以退出码 3 fail
    closed（0 成功 / 2 NO_KEY / 3 泄漏）。
  - **P2 北交所映射**：`exchange_for_ts_code` 改为 SH→SSE、SZ→SZSE、BJ→BJSE，
    未知后缀抛 ValueError fail closed（原逻辑 `.BJ` 误判 SSE）。

    路径回退绝对路径字符串，不再抛异常。
- 附带加固：`annotate_row_cap_hits` 不再限定 market_window_filtered scope——任意
  endpoint 命中行数上限都标注 `row_cap_suspected`（防止上游忽略过滤参数时失明）。

  变化，其余 22 项 digest 完全一致；产物对真实 Token 包含性扫描 clean。运行档案与

## 2026-08-16 | TA-TUSHARE-2000-001E：退避、可审计缓存与动作降级收口（P1）

- `CnTushareProvider` 限流有界退避：仅 RATE_LIMITED 触发重试，指数退避单次
  sleep 封顶（默认 8s）、总次数封顶（默认 3 次），权限拒绝/通用失败绝不重试；
  退避可注入（sleep_fn）可配置，非法配置构造即拒绝；`attempts`/`retry_reason`
  （rate_limit_backoff_recovered/exhausted）进入 001B 结构化审计。
- 可审计缓存：条目升级为 (monotonic, wall_time, frame)，`cache_audit_records()`
  输出 endpoint/参数/查询时间/数据期间/TTL/age/expired 的脱敏审计；旧 2 元组
  条目向后兼容。新增 `read_cache()` STALE 只读出口——永不触发上游调用，新鲜
  命中返回 HAS_DATA（cache.hit），过期条目返回 STALE（stale_served=true，保留
  旧帧行数/哈希但不冒充最新）。
- 契约扩展：`TushareQueryResult` 增加 attempts/retry_reason 并进入 to_dict。
- 动作降级集成链（新增专项测试证明，健康基线对照）：Tushare 关键证据失败
  （资金流限流耗尽、两融/回购权限拒绝）→ raw_evidence FAILED →
  evidence_coverage < 70 → 强动作门禁不通过 + Buy Level 受限 → D-002 清洗器
  真实移除最终响应中的"立即买入"，而非只追加警告。
- 真实小范围运行（603629.SH，24 endpoint：财务 9 + 治理 8 + 市场行为 7，
  0.6s 间隔）：全部成功（HAS_DATA 22 + NORMAL_NO_DATA 2——express 无快报、
  top_list 非上榜日），未触发限流重试；真实 pack 全文只写 /tmp 调用方目录，
  run archive 只存哈希与元数据 + 9 项仍未知接口清单。凭据扫描 clean。
- 验证：新增专项 **16 passed**；Tushare 相关回归 **393 passed**；readiness/
  collector 回归 **770 passed**。运行档案
  `docs/task_runs/TA-TUSHARE-2000-001E-20260816-095043/`。

## 2026-08-16 | TA-TUSHARE-2000-001B：Tushare 统一八态错误契约（P0）

- 新增 `tradingagents/dataflows/tushare_query_contract.py`：Tushare endpoint 查询
  统一八态结构化结果（HAS_DATA/NORMAL_NO_DATA/QUERY_FAILED/PERMISSION_DENIED/
  RATE_LIMITED/FIELD_MISSING/NOT_QUERIED/STALE），复用 001A 的脱敏、哈希与日期
  范围原语；每条结果携带 endpoint、参数摘要、query time、data period、response
  SHA-256、cache metadata 与脱敏 error，`to_dict()` 可直接 JSON 审计。
- 契约 fail-closed 校验：QUERY_FAILED/PERMISSION_DENIED/RATE_LIMITED/NOT_QUERIED
  不得携带数值 row_count/data_period（禁止把失败写成 0）；FIELD_MISSING 必须列出
  missing_fields 且 row_count ≥ 1；STALE 必须附带过期帧证据；row_count 必须与
  附带 frame 一致；合法业务数值 0 继续保留在数据帧中不被丢弃。
- `cn_tushare_provider` 全部查询改经 `_run_structured_query`：真实状态写入有界
  （128 条）审计队列，`structured_results()`/`last_structured_result()` 为审计
  真源；`_optional_query` 的"权限/限流/失败一律空表"崩塌被消除；上游失败 +
  过期缓存副本 → 审计记 STALE 不冒充最新；本地未配置 → NOT_QUERIED 且
  upstream_called=false；lhb force=False → NOT_QUERIED；moneyflow 缺大单字段 →
  FIELD_MISSING。兼容文本出口（财务/资金流/龙虎榜/两融/回购）与异常语义逐条
  保持不变（既有 84 项 provider/capability 回归全绿）。
- 验证：新增契约专项 **33 passed**（八态全覆盖 + 权限/限流/空数据/字段缺失互不
  误判 + token 全链路脱敏 + 缓存元数据/STALE/审计有界）；Tushare 相关回归合计
  **334 passed**。凭据自检：新增文件对真实 TUSHARE_TOKEN（HAS_KEY）包含性检查
  CLEAN，产物只含 HAS_KEY/NO_KEY 级别状态。运行档案
  `docs/task_runs/TA-TUSHARE-2000-001B-20260816-092845/`。未调用 live LLM、未写
  生产数据库、未做真实网络查询。

## 2026-08-16 | TA-TUSHARE-2000-001A：Tushare 2000 积分真实权限矩阵（P0）

- 新增 `tradingagents/dataflows/tushare_capability.py`：任务范围 24 个 endpoint 的声明式注册表
  （财务 9 / 治理事件 8 / 市场行为 7，外加 9 项明确排除的 unknown 能力）、查询结果五态分类
  （HAS_DATA/NORMAL_NO_DATA/PERMISSION_DENIED/RATE_LIMITED/QUERY_FAILED + NOT_QUERIED）、
  凭据脱敏清洗（Token 子串、Authorization/Cookie/Session 头、40+ 位不透明 blob）、
  响应 SHA-256 与日期范围提取、单探测+受控重试执行器（权限拒绝与限流绝不重试；
  仅瞬态网络错误或日期型空结果允许一次带原因重试）、矩阵 JSON/Markdown 渲染与 2000 行
  上限命中自动标注。模块不读取静态 `SOURCE_CAPABILITY_MATRIX`。
- 新增 `scripts/audit_tushare_capability.py`：Token 只从 git-ignored `.env` 读取，
  NO_KEY 一律 fail closed（退出码 2、零探测）；`--dry-run` 不发网络请求；探测间隔 0.6s。
- 真实运行（603629.SH，探测日 20260814）：24/24 endpoint 全部 allowed——22 HAS_DATA +
  2 NORMAL_NO_DATA（express 窗口内无快报、top_list 非上榜日且回退日重试一次有原因记录）；
  `repurchase` 市场窗口单次查询命中 2000 行上限，已标注不影响权限结论、不代表窗口完整覆盖。
  `PERMISSION_DENIED/QUERY_FAILED/RATE_LIMITED` 未在真实运行触发，按任务要求以脱敏 fixture
  证据（`tests/fixtures/tushare_capability/`）标注为未验证。
- 产物：`docs/task_runs/TA-TUSHARE-2000-001A-20260816-041709/` 下机器可读 JSON、人读
  Markdown 矩阵与运行回执；不含真实响应正文、Token、Cookie 或 Authorization，凭据自检
  clean。静态能力矩阵未被当作本任务真实结果。

## 2026-07-31 | 自动财务事实导出与历史时点防穿越

- 新增跨源财务事实导出：公司身份、利润表、现金流量表和资产负债表统一为可追溯
  bundle，按底层来源去重并保留 provider、source_id、原始值、期间口径和派生公式。
- 新增东方财富财报适配器，与现有新浪财报链交叉校验；交叉一致时保留真实来源值，
  不取平均制造第三个数值，重大差异标记冲突。
- 东方财富实时财报接口只证明当前观察到的 payload，不再用原始 `NOTICE_DATE`
  回填历史可见性；历史 `as_of` 必须读取当时归档的不可变 bundle。来源没有可验证
  公告版本时，以本次实际观察日作为最早可用日，并标记
  `disclosure_date_inferred=true`。
- bundle 入口统一校验并规范化 A 股代码与交易所后缀；跨源一致性逐项比较真实来源值，
  不再先按来源求平均；导出命令只有在至少一条事实达到跨源验证时才返回成功。
- 600487.SH 实盘回放得到 1733 条事实：92 条跨源一致、1641 条单源、0 冲突；
  2026Q1 营业收入取东方财富真实值 17,790,560,069.79 元。公司身份目前仍只有
  Eastmoney 一个独立底层来源，因此知识库侧继续按待核验处理。
- 历史 `as_of` 不再调用任何实时身份或报表接口，必须改读当时归档的不可变 bundle；
  资产负债表的固定资产字段也已进入规范化事实与证据链；母公司权益与所有者权益
  总额分开建模，公司名称只允许可解释的法定名称转换，身份源全失败保持
  `QUERY_FAILED`。
- 归母净利润计算 ROE 时优先匹配归母权益，仅在归母权益缺失时回退所有者权益
  总额；固定资产同时兼容“固定资产”和旧接口的“固定资产净额”字段。
- 东方财富成功响应若缺少 result、data 或合法分页信息，统一按查询失败处理，
  不再伪装为正常无数据或静默截断历史；异常文本会清洗带厂商前缀的 API key、
  token、secret、cookie 和 session 等凭据。接口提供的真实公告日现可被识别，
  600487.SH 实盘事实中 1641 条不再误标为推断披露日。
- 东方财富每条返回记录必须带有并匹配请求证券代码，畸形记录或混入其他发行人时
  整次查询失败；身份共识也会保留任一供应商返回的 `IDENTITY_CONFLICT`，不允许
  后续同名结果把真实冲突覆盖掉。
- 验证：FUND、财务事实与财务异常聚焦回归 **414 passed**；Python `py_compile` 与
  `git diff --check` 通过。未调用 LLM、未写生产数据库、未修改 prompts。

### 降级审计契约补修

- 单源、正常无数据、冲突和部分 provider 查询失败仍写入不可变 bundle，但只能
  使用带时间戳的 retryable 审计旁路，不能占用正式验证输出路径。
- 降级结果继续返回非零退出码，并显式携带 `verified=false`，避免下游把“已有审计
  文件”误读为“已经跨源核验”。
- bundle 保留实际观察日期，供知识库侧阻断历史时点穿越；后续健康重试可以在不
  覆盖旧审计记录的前提下写入正式验证结果。
- 聚焦回归覆盖 exporter、bundle、东财 provider、期间标准化、证据契约和 readiness：
  **346 passed**。

### 独立发行人身份核验补修

- 默认财务导出增加只负责身份核验的巨潮 provider，公司代码与名称不再由两个共享
  同一上游资料源的 adapter 伪装成双源一致。
- 混合主源/fallback 资料只计一个身份血缘；每个被归因的数据段都必须返回六位证券
  代码和证券简称。巨潮响应缺证券代码时 fail closed，不回填请求参数冒充响应值。
- 600487.SH 真实回放曾返回 `HAS_DATA`：巨潮与东财身份一致、92 条跨源财务事实、
  0 条冲突。身份及财务契约聚焦回归 **296 passed**，静态编译和 `git diff --check`
  通过。

+def resolve_token(
+    *,
+    environ: Mapping[str, str],
+    dotenv_path: Path | None,
+    allow_dotenv: bool = True,
+) -> tuple[str, str, bool]:
+    """Resolve TUSHARE_TOKEN with explicit, auditable precedence.
+
+    001A-R1 fix (c73ed4f P1#1): the explicitly specified git-ignored ``.env``
+    file always wins over an inherited process environment variable, so the
+    permission matrix can never be silently attributed to a foreign token.
+    Returns ``(token, token_source, env_override_applied)`` where source is
+    ``dotenv_file``/``inherited_environment``/``""`` (NO_KEY).
+    """
+
+    dotenv_token = ""
+    if allow_dotenv and dotenv_path is not None:
+        try:
+            values: Mapping[str, str | None] = dotenv_values(dotenv_path)
+        except OSError:
+            values = {}
+        dotenv_token = str(values.get("TUSHARE_TOKEN", "") or "").strip()
+    inherited = str(environ.get("TUSHARE_TOKEN", "") or "").strip()
+    if dotenv_token:
+        overridden = bool(inherited) and inherited != dotenv_token
+        return dotenv_token, TOKEN_SOURCE_DOTENV, overridden
+    if inherited:
+        return inherited, TOKEN_SOURCE_INHERITED_ENV, False
+    return "", "", False
+
+
+def display_path(path: Path, *, root: Path = ROOT) -> str:
+    """Relative display path that never crashes for paths outside root."""
+
+    try:
+        return str(path.resolve().relative_to(root.resolve()))
+    except ValueError:
+        return str(path)
+

+def scan_artifacts_for_secret(
+    texts: Mapping[str, str], token: str
+) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
+    """Return ``(artifact_name, reason)`` leak findings; empty means clean."""
+
+    leaks: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
+    for name, text in texts.items():
+        if token and token in text:
+            leaks.append((name, "token_substring"))
+            continue
+        lowered = text.lower()
+        if "authorization:" in lowered:
+            leaks.append((name, "authorization_header"))
+        if "cookie:" in lowered:
+            leaks.append((name, "cookie_header"))
+    return leaks

+def _load_audit_module():
+    """Import the audit CLI as a module without invoking ``__main__``."""
+    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("audit_tushare_capability", SCRIPT)
+    assert spec and spec.loader, "unable to build spec for audit module"
+    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
+    return module

+    def test_resolve_token_precedence_matrix(self, tmp_path):
+        audit = _load_audit_module()
+        dotenv_file = tmp_path / ".env"
+        dotenv_file.write_text("TUSHARE_TOKEN=dotenv-token\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        resolved = audit.resolve_token(
+            environ={"TUSHARE_TOKEN": "env-token"}, dotenv_path=dotenv_file
+        )
+        assert resolved == ("dotenv-token", TOKEN_SOURCE_DOTENV, True)
+        same = audit.resolve_token(
+            environ={"TUSHARE_TOKEN": "dotenv-token"}, dotenv_path=dotenv_file
+        )
+        assert same == ("dotenv-token", TOKEN_SOURCE_DOTENV, False)
+        inherited = audit.resolve_token(
+            environ={"TUSHARE_TOKEN": "env-token"}, dotenv_path=dotenv_file, allow_dotenv=False
+        )
+        assert inherited == ("env-token", TOKEN_SOURCE_INHERITED_ENV, False)
+        empty_dotenv = tmp_path / "empty.env"
+        empty_dotenv.write_text("OTHER=1\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        fallback = audit.resolve_token(environ={"TUSHARE_TOKEN": "env-token"}, dotenv_path=empty_dotenv)
+        assert fallback == ("env-token", TOKEN_SOURCE_INHERITED_ENV, False)
+        missing = audit.resolve_token(environ={}, dotenv_path=tmp_path / "absent.env")
+        assert missing == ("", "", False)

+def resolve_token(
+    *,
+    environ: Mapping[str, str],
+    dotenv_path: Path | None,
+    allow_dotenv: bool = True,
+) -> tuple[str, str, bool]:
+    """Resolve TUSHARE_TOKEN with explicit, auditable precedence.
+
+    001A-R1 fix (c73ed4f P1#1): the explicitly specified git-ignored ``.env``
+    file always wins over an inherited process environment variable, so the
+    permission matrix can never be silently attributed to a foreign token.
+    Returns ``(token, token_source, env_override_applied)`` where source is
+    ``dotenv_file``/``inherited_environment``/``""`` (NO_KEY).
+    """
+
+    dotenv_token = ""
+    if allow_dotenv and dotenv_path is not None:
+        try:
+            values: Mapping[str, str | None] = dotenv_values(dotenv_path)
+        except OSError:
+            values = {}
+        dotenv_token = str(values.get("TUSHARE_TOKEN", "") or "").strip()
+    inherited = str(environ.get("TUSHARE_TOKEN", "") or "").strip()
+    if dotenv_token:
+        overridden = bool(inherited) and inherited != dotenv_token
+        return dotenv_token, TOKEN_SOURCE_DOTENV, overridden
+    if inherited:
+        return inherited, TOKEN_SOURCE_INHERITED_ENV, False
+    return "", "", False
+
+
+def display_path(path: Path, *, root: Path = ROOT) -> str:
+    """Relative display path that never crashes for paths outside root."""
+
+    try:
+        return str(path.resolve().relative_to(root.resolve()))
+    except ValueError:
+        return str(path)
+
+
 def build_probe_context(args: argparse.Namespace) -> dict[str, str]:
     ts_code = normalize_symbol(args.symbol)
     return {
         "ts_code": ts_code,

+def scan_artifacts_for_secret(
+    texts: Mapping[str, str], token: str
+) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
+    """Return ``(artifact_name, reason)`` leak findings; empty means clean."""
+
+    leaks: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
+    for name, text in texts.items():
+        if token and token in text:
+            leaks.append((name, "token_substring"))
+            continue
+        lowered = text.lower()
+        if "authorization:" in lowered:
+            leaks.append((name, "authorization_header"))
+        if "cookie:" in lowered:
+            leaks.append((name, "cookie_header"))
+    return leaks
+
+

+    def test_dotenv_token_overrides_inherited_env(self, tmp_path):
+        dotenv_file = tmp_path / "custom.env"
+        dotenv_file.write_text("TUSHARE_TOKEN=token-from-dotenv\n", encoding="utf-8")

+            [
+                sys.executable,
+                str(SCRIPT),
+                "--dry-run",
+                "--dotenv",
+                str(dotenv_file),
+                "--output-dir",
+                str(tmp_path / "out"),
+            ],
+            capture_output=True,
+            text=True,
+            env={"PATH": "/usr/bin:/bin", "TUSHARE_TOKEN": "token-from-inherited-env"},
+            cwd=str(ROOT),

+        assert "env_override_applied=true" in result.stdout

+        assert "token-from-inherited-env" not in result.stdout
+
+    def test_resolve_token_precedence_matrix(self, tmp_path):
+        audit = _load_audit_module()
+        dotenv_file = tmp_path / ".env"
+        dotenv_file.write_text("TUSHARE_TOKEN=dotenv-token\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        resolved = audit.resolve_token(
+            environ={"TUSHARE_TOKEN": "env-token"}, dotenv_path=dotenv_file
+        )
+        assert resolved == ("dotenv-token", TOKEN_SOURCE_DOTENV, True)
+        same = audit.resolve_token(
+            environ={"TUSHARE_TOKEN": "dotenv-token"}, dotenv_path=dotenv_file
+        )
+        assert same == ("dotenv-token", TOKEN_SOURCE_DOTENV, False)
+        inherited = audit.resolve_token(
+            environ={"TUSHARE_TOKEN": "env-token"}, dotenv_path=dotenv_file, allow_dotenv=False
+        )
+        assert inherited == ("env-token", TOKEN_SOURCE_INHERITED_ENV, False)
+        empty_dotenv = tmp_path / "empty.env"
+        empty_dotenv.write_text("OTHER=1\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        fallback = audit.resolve_token(environ={"TUSHARE_TOKEN": "env-token"}, dotenv_path=empty_dotenv)
+        assert fallback == ("env-token", TOKEN_SOURCE_INHERITED_ENV, False)
+        missing = audit.resolve_token(environ={}, dotenv_path=tmp_path / "absent.env")
+        assert missing == ("", "", False)
+
+    def test_exchange_mapping_covers_bj_and_fails_closed_on_unknown(self):

+    def test_write_outputs_clean_matrix_writes_all_artifacts(self, tmp_path):
+        audit = _load_audit_module()

+            for spec in ENDPOINT_SPECS

     1	"""TA-TUSHARE-2000-001A: real Tushare 2000-point permission matrix audit.
     2	
     3	Runs one minimal, single-stock, low-frequency probe per endpoint listed in
     4	the task scope and writes a sanitized permission matrix (JSON + Markdown)
     5	plus a run receipt into a ``docs/task_runs/TA-TUSHARE-2000-001A-*``
     6	archive.  The Tushare token is read exclusively from the git-ignored
     7	``.env`` file (or, with ``--no-dotenv``, from the inherited environment for
     8	test isolation only); every artifact only records ``HAS_KEY``/``NO_KEY``.
     9	
    10	Exit codes: 0 success; 2 NO_KEY fail-closed; 3 credential leak detected in
    11	composed artifacts (nothing is written, fail closed).
    12	"""
    13

    20	from datetime import datetime
    21	from pathlib import Path

    25	if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:

    51	FIXTURES_DIR = ROOT / "tests" / "fixtures" / "tushare_capability"

    53	MATRIX_MD_NAME = "tushare_permission_matrix.md"

    57	_SUFFIX_TO_EXCHANGE = {"SH": "SSE", "SZ": "SZSE", "BJ": "BJSE"}
    58	
    59	
    60	def exchange_for_ts_code(ts_code: str) -> str:
    61	    suffix = ts_code.rsplit(".", 1)[-1].upper()
    62	    try:
    63	        return _SUFFIX_TO_EXCHANGE[suffix]
    64	    except KeyError:
    65	        raise ValueError(f"unknown exchange suffix in ts_code: {ts_code!r}") from None
    66	
    67	
    68	def normalize_symbol(symbol: str) -> str:
    69	    from tradingagents.dataflows.providers.cn_tushare_provider import _normalize_ts_code
    70	
    71	    return _normalize_ts_code(symbol)

    73	
    74	def resolve_token(
    75	    *,
    76	    environ: Mapping[str, str],
    77	    dotenv_path: Path | None,
    78	    allow_dotenv: bool = True,
    79	) -> tuple[str, str, bool]:
    80	    """Resolve TUSHARE_TOKEN with explicit, auditable precedence.
    81	
    82	    001A-R1 fix (c73ed4f P1#1): the explicitly specified git-ignored ``.env``
    83	    file always wins over an inherited process environment variable, so the
    84	    permission matrix can never be silently attributed to a foreign token.
    85	    Returns ``(token, token_source, env_override_applied)`` where source is
    86	    ``dotenv_file``/``inherited_environment``/``""`` (NO_KEY).
    87	    """
    88	
    89	    dotenv_token = ""
    90	    if allow_dotenv and dotenv_path is not None:
    91	        try:
    92	            values: Mapping[str, str | None] = dotenv_values(dotenv_path)
    93	        except OSError:
    94	            values = {}
    95	        dotenv_token = str(values.get("TUSHARE_TOKEN", "") or "").strip()
    96	    inherited = str(environ.get("TUSHARE_TOKEN", "") or "").strip()
    97	    if dotenv_token:
    98	        overridden = bool(inherited) and inherited != dotenv_token
    99	        return dotenv_token, TOKEN_SOURCE_DOTENV, overridden
   100	    if inherited:
   101	        return inherited, TOKEN_SOURCE_INHERITED_ENV, False
   102	    return "", "", False
   103	
   104	
   105	def display_path(path: Path, *, root: Path = ROOT) -> str:
   106	    """Relative display path that never crashes for paths outside root."""
   107	
   108	    try:
   109	        return str(path.resolve().relative_to(root.resolve()))
   110	    except ValueError:
   111	        return str(path)
   112	
   113	
   114	def build_probe_context(args: argparse.Namespace) -> dict[str, str]:
   115	    ts_code = normalize_symbol(args.symbol)
   116	    return {
   117	        "ts_code": ts_code,
   118	        "exchange": exchange_for_ts_code(ts_code),
   119	        "probe_date": args.probe_date,
   120	        "fallback_date": args.fallback_date,
   121	        "window_start": args.window_start,
   122	        "statement_start": args.statement_start,
   123	    }
   124	
   125	
   126	def state_evidence_from_fixtures() -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   127	    evidence: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
   128	    if not FIXTURES_DIR.is_dir():
   129	        return evidence
   130	    for path in sorted(FIXTURES_DIR.glob("*.json")):
   131	        try:
   132	            payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   133	        except (OSError, ValueError):
   134	            continue
   135	        state = payload.get("expected_state")
   136	        if state:
   137	            evidence[str(state)] = {
   138	                "fixture": f"tests/fixtures/tushare_capability/{path.name}",
   139	                "fixture_kind": payload.get("fixture_kind", "sanitized"),
   140	            }
   141	    return evidence
   142	
   143	
   144	def make_query_fn(token: str):
   145	    import tushare as ts
   146	
   147	    client = ts.pro_api(token)
   148	
   149	    def query(endpoint: str, **kwargs: Any):
   150	        return client.query(endpoint, **kwargs)
   151	
   152	    return query
   153	
   154	
   155	def scan_artifacts_for_secret(
   156	    texts: Mapping[str, str], token: str
   157	) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   158	    """Return ``(artifact_name, reason)`` leak findings; empty means clean."""
   159	
   160	    leaks: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
   161	    for name, text in texts.items():
   162	        if token and token in text:
   163	            leaks.append((name, "token_substring"))
   164	            continue
   165	        lowered = text.lower()
   166	        if "authorization:" in lowered:
   167	            leaks.append((name, "authorization_header"))
   168	        if "cookie:" in lowered:
   169	            leaks.append((name, "cookie_header"))
   170	    return leaks
   171	
   172	
   173	def run_probes(query_fn, probe_context: Mapping[str, str], *, token: str, sleep_seconds: float):
   174	    records = []
   175	    for index, spec in enumerate(ENDPOINT_SPECS):
   176	        if index:
   177	            time.sleep(sleep_seconds)
   178	        try:
   179	            record = probe_endpoint(query_fn, spec, probe_context, token=token)
   180	        except Exception as exc:
   181	            record = not_queried_record(
   182	                spec,
   183	                probe_context,
   184	                f"probe runner unexpected failure: {sanitize_error_text(exc, token)}",
   185	            )
   186	        records.append(record)
   187	        print(
   188	            f"[probe] {record['endpoint']:<20} {record['state']:<18} "
   189	            f"rows={record.get('row_count') if record.get('row_count') is not None else '-'} "
   190	            f"attempts={record.get('attempts', 1)}",
   191	            flush=True,
   192	        )
   193	    return records
   194	
   195	
   196	def write_outputs(
   197	    matrix: dict[str, Any],
   198	    output_dir: Path,
   199	    *,
   200	    token: str,
   201	    token_source: str = "",
   202	    env_override_applied: bool = False,
   203	) -> tuple[list[Path], list[tuple[str, str]]]:
   204	    """Compose, scan, then write artifacts. Fail closed on credential leaks.
   205	
   206	    001A-R1 fix (c73ed4f P1#3): when any composed artifact trips the
   207	    credential scan, nothing is written and the leak findings are returned
   208	    so the caller exits non-zero.
   209	    """
   210	
   211	    json_text = json.dumps(matrix, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=False) + "\n"
   212	    markdown = render_markdown(matrix)
   213	    leaks = scan_artifacts_for_secret(
   214	        {MATRIX_JSON_NAME: json_text, MATRIX_MD_NAME: markdown}, token
   215	    )
   216	    if leaks:
   217	        return [], leaks
   218	    source_label = (
   219	        "git-ignored .env 文件（TUSHARE_TOKEN）"
   220	        if token_source == TOKEN_SOURCE_DOTENV
   221	        else "继承环境变量（TUSHARE_TOKEN）"
   222	        if token_source == TOKEN_SOURCE_INHERITED_ENV
   223	        else "未知来源"
   224	    )
   225	    retry_lines = [
   226	        f"- `{record['endpoint']}`：attempts={record['attempts']}，reason=`{record['retry_reason']}`"
   227	        for record in matrix["endpoints"]
   228	        if record.get("attempts", 1) > 1
   229	    ]
   230	    repurchase_lines = ["- 本矩阵未包含 repurchase 记录"]
   231	    for record in matrix["endpoints"]:
   232	        if record["endpoint"] == "repurchase":
   233	            repurchase_lines = [
   234	                f"- 查询参数：`{json.dumps(record.get('params', {}), ensure_ascii=False)}`",
   235	                f"- 返回行数：{record.get('row_count') if record.get('row_count') is not None else '-'}"
   236	                f"（state=`{record['state']}`）",
   237	                f"- scope：`{record.get('scope')}`；row_cap_suspected="
   238	                f"`{record.get('row_cap_suspected', False)}`",
   239	            ]
   240	            break
   241	    token_source_line = (
   242	        f"- Token 来源：{source_label}，来源标识 `{token_source or 'unknown'}`，"
   243	        f"状态 `{matrix['token_status']}`，任何产物不包含 Token 明文"
   244	    )
   245	    if env_override_applied:
   246	        token_source_line += (
   247	            "；继承环境变量中的 TUSHARE_TOKEN 已被显式 .env 值覆盖（env_override_applied=true）"
   248	        )
   249	    receipt = [
   250	        f"# Run receipt — {TASK_ID}",
   251	        "",
   252	        f"- 运行时间：{matrix['generated_at']}",
   253	        token_source_line,
   254	        f"- 探测标的：`{matrix['probe']['symbol']}`；探测日 `{matrix['probe']['probe_date']}`；"
   255	        f"回退日 `{matrix['probe']['fallback_date']}`",
   256	        f"- 实测 endpoint 数：{matrix['summary']['probed_endpoints']}；"
   257	        f"状态分布：{json.dumps(matrix['summary']['by_state'], ensure_ascii=False)}",
   258	        "",
   259	        "## 重试记录",
   260	        "",
   261	        *(retry_lines or ["- 无重试"]),
   262	        "",
   263	        "## repurchase 单股查询记录（001A-R1）",
   264	        "",
   265	        *repurchase_lines,
   266	        "",
   267	        "## 凭据自检",
   268	        "",
   269	        f"- 对 {MATRIX_JSON_NAME}、{MATRIX_MD_NAME} 与本回执扫描 Token/Authorization/Cookie：clean"
   270	        "（发现泄漏即拒绝写盘并以退出码 3 fail closed）",
   271	        "- 本回执与矩阵均不含真实响应正文，只含结构化元数据与 SHA-256 摘要",
   272	        "",
   273	    ]
   274	    receipt_text = "\n".join(receipt)
   275	    receipt_leaks = scan_artifacts_for_secret({RECEIPT_NAME: receipt_text}, token)
   276	    if receipt_leaks:
   277	        return [], receipt_leaks
   278	    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   279	    json_path = output_dir / MATRIX_JSON_NAME
   280	    md_path = output_dir / MATRIX_MD_NAME
   281	    receipt_path = output_dir / RECEIPT_NAME
   282	    json_path.write_text(json_text, encoding="utf-8")
   283	    md_path.write_text(markdown, encoding="utf-8")
   284	    receipt_path.write_text(receipt_text, encoding="utf-8")
   285	    return [json_path, md_path, receipt_path], []
   286	
   287	
   288	def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
   289	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Tushare 2000-point real permission matrix audit")
   290	    parser.add_argument("--symbol", default=DEFAULT_SYMBOL)
   291	    parser.add_argument("--probe-date", default=DEFAULT_PROBE_DATE)
   292	    parser.add_argument("--fallback-date", default=DEFAULT_FALLBACK_DATE)
   293	    parser.add_argument("--window-start", default=DEFAULT_WINDOW_START)
   294	    parser.add_argument("--statement-start", default=DEFAULT_STATEMENT_START)
   295	    parser.add_argument("--sleep-seconds", type=float, default=0.6)
   296	    parser.add_argument(
   297	        "--output-dir",
   298	        default=None,
   299	        help="default: docs/task_runs/TA-TUSHARE-2000-001A-<YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS>",
   300	    )
   301	    parser.add_argument(
   302	        "--dotenv",
   303	        default=None,
   304	        help="显式指定 .env 文件路径（默认 <repo>/.env）；该文件的 TUSHARE_TOKEN 优先于继承环境变量",
   305	    )
   306	    parser.add_argument(
   307	        "--no-dotenv",
   308	        action="store_true",
   309	        help="跳过 .env 加载（仅供测试隔离使用；Token 只能来自环境，来源将记录为继承环境变量）",
   310	    )
   311	    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="打印探测计划，不发起网络请求")
   312	    return parser
   313	
   314	
   315	def main(
   316	    argv: list[str] | None = None,
   317	    *,
   318	    environ: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,
   319	    dotenv_path: Path | None = None,
   320	) -> int:
   321	    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
   322	    import os
   323	
   324	    env = os.environ if environ is None else environ
   325	    resolved_dotenv = (
   326	        Path(args.dotenv)
   327	        if args.dotenv
   328	        else (dotenv_path if dotenv_path is not None else ROOT / ".env")
   329	    )
   330	    token, token_source, env_override_applied = resolve_token(
   331	        environ=env,
   332	        dotenv_path=resolved_dotenv,
   333	        allow_dotenv=not args.no_dotenv,
   334	    )
   335	    token_status = TOKEN_STATUS_HAS_KEY if token else TOKEN_STATUS_NO_KEY
   336	    probe_context = build_probe_context(args)
   337	    if token_status == TOKEN_STATUS_NO_KEY:
   338	        print(
   339	            "[audit] TUSHARE_TOKEN NO_KEY：拒绝探测（fail closed），"
   340	            f"Token 只允许来自 git-ignored .env（本次来源解析：{resolved_dotenv}）"
   341	        )
   342	        return 2
   343	    if args.dry_run:
   344	        print(
   345	            f"[audit] token={token_status} token_source={token_source or 'unknown'} "
   346	            f"env_override_applied={str(env_override_applied).lower()} "
   347	            f"symbol={probe_context['ts_code']} probe_date={args.probe_date}"
   348	        )
   349	        for spec in ENDPOINT_SPECS:
   350	            print(f"[dry-run] {spec.endpoint:<20} category={spec.category:<10} kinds={spec.param_kinds}")
   351	        print(f"[dry-run] excluded items: {len(EXCLUDED_ENDPOINTS)}")
   352	        return 0
   353	    started = datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   354	    stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
   355	    output_dir = (
   356	        Path(args.output_dir)
   357	        if args.output_dir
   358	        else ROOT / "docs" / "task_runs" / f"{TASK_ID}-{stamp}"
   359	    )
   360	    print(
   361	        f"[audit] token={token_status} token_source={token_source or 'unknown'} "
   362	        f"env_override_applied={str(env_override_applied).lower()} "
   363	        f"symbol={probe_context['ts_code']} "
   364	        f"probe_date={args.probe_date} output={display_path(output_dir)}"
   365	    )
   366	    query_fn = make_query_fn(token)
   367	    records = run_probes(query_fn, probe_context, token=token, sleep_seconds=args.sleep_seconds)
   368	    matrix = build_matrix(
   369	        probe_context,
   370	        records,
   371	        token_status=token_status,
   372	        token_source=token_source,
   373	        token_env_override_applied=env_override_applied,
   374	        generated_at=started,
   375	        state_evidence=state_evidence_from_fixtures(),
   376	        notes=[
   377	            f"探测脚本：scripts/audit_tushare_capability.py，探测间隔 {args.sleep_seconds}s",
   378	            f"Token 来源标识：{token_source or 'unknown'}（显式解析并记录，杜绝继承环境变量静默覆盖）",
   379	        ],
   380	    )
   381	    annotate_row_cap_hits(matrix)
   382	    written, leaks = write_outputs(
   383	        matrix,
   384	        output_dir,
   385	        token=token,
   386	        token_source=token_source,
   387	        env_override_applied=env_override_applied,
   388	    )
   389	    if leaks:
   390	        for name, reason in leaks:
   391	            print(f"[audit] credential leak detected in {name} ({reason})")
   392	        print("[audit] 拒绝写盘并 fail closed（退出码 3）")
   393	        return 3
   394	    print(f"[audit] wrote {', '.join(display_path(path) for path in written)}")
   395	    return 0
   396	
   397	
   398	if __name__ == "__main__":
   399	    raise SystemExit(main())

    82	_DATE_RANGE_FIELD_PRIORITY = ("trade_date", "ann_date", "f_ann_date", "end_date")

    84	_CREDENTIAL_KEY_RE = re.compile(
    85	    r"(?i)\b(authorization|cookie|token|secret|api[_-]?key|password|session)"
    86	    r"(\s*[:=]\s*)((?:bearer\s+)?[^\s,;'\"]+)"
    87	)
    88	_OPAQUE_BLOB_RE = re.compile(r"\b[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{40,}\b")

    92	PARAM_KIND_RECENT_WINDOW = "recent_window"
    93	PARAM_KIND_PROBE_DATE = "probe_date"
    94	PARAM_KIND_PROBE_DATE_WITH_SYMBOL = "probe_date_with_symbol"
    95	PARAM_KIND_EXCHANGE_PROBE_DATE = "exchange_probe_date"
    96	
    97	ROW_CAP_DEFAULT = 2000
    98	_ROW_CAP_NOTE = (
    99	    "endpoint 单次探测命中 {cap} 行返回上限：权限结论（allowed）不受影响，但该行数不代表"
   100	    "完整窗口或最小单股结果，需核对查询参数是否被上游忽略"
   101	)

   104	@dataclass(frozen=True)
   105	class EndpointSpec:
   106	    endpoint: str
   107	    category: str
   108	    param_kinds: tuple[str, ...]
   109	    scope: str = "symbol"
   110	    date_bound: bool = False
   111	    note: str = ""
   112	
   113	
   114	ENDPOINT_SPECS: tuple[EndpointSpec, ...] = (
   115	    EndpointSpec("income", CATEGORY_FINANCIAL, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE, PARAM_KIND_STATEMENT_WINDOW)),
   116	    EndpointSpec("balancesheet", CATEGORY_FINANCIAL, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE, PARAM_KIND_STATEMENT_WINDOW)),
   117	    EndpointSpec("cashflow", CATEGORY_FINANCIAL, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE, PARAM_KIND_STATEMENT_WINDOW)),
   118	    EndpointSpec("fina_indicator", CATEGORY_FINANCIAL, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE, PARAM_KIND_STATEMENT_WINDOW)),
   119	    EndpointSpec("forecast", CATEGORY_FINANCIAL, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE, PARAM_KIND_STATEMENT_WINDOW)),
   120	    EndpointSpec("express", CATEGORY_FINANCIAL, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE, PARAM_KIND_STATEMENT_WINDOW)),
   121	    EndpointSpec("dividend", CATEGORY_FINANCIAL, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE,)),
   122	    EndpointSpec("fina_audit", CATEGORY_FINANCIAL, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE,)),
   123	    EndpointSpec("fina_mainbz", CATEGORY_FINANCIAL, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE,)),
   124	    EndpointSpec("top10_holders", CATEGORY_GOVERNANCE, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE,)),
   125	    EndpointSpec("top10_floatholders", CATEGORY_GOVERNANCE, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE,)),
   126	    EndpointSpec("pledge_stat", CATEGORY_GOVERNANCE, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE,)),
   127	    EndpointSpec("pledge_detail", CATEGORY_GOVERNANCE, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE,)),
   128	    EndpointSpec(
   129	        "repurchase",
   130	        CATEGORY_GOVERNANCE,
   131	        (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE,),
   132	        note="repurchase 单 ts_code 最小查询（001A-R1 修复：原公告日窗口查询命中全市场行数上限，违反单股最小探测约束）",
   133	    ),
   134	    EndpointSpec("share_float", CATEGORY_GOVERNANCE, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE,)),
   135	    EndpointSpec("stk_holdernumber", CATEGORY_GOVERNANCE, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE,)),
   136	    EndpointSpec("stk_holdertrade", CATEGORY_GOVERNANCE, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE,)),
   137	    EndpointSpec("daily", CATEGORY_MARKET, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE, PARAM_KIND_RECENT_WINDOW)),
   138	    EndpointSpec("adj_factor", CATEGORY_MARKET, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE, PARAM_KIND_RECENT_WINDOW)),
   139	    EndpointSpec("daily_basic", CATEGORY_MARKET, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE, PARAM_KIND_RECENT_WINDOW)),
   140	    EndpointSpec("moneyflow", CATEGORY_MARKET, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE, PARAM_KIND_RECENT_WINDOW)),
   141	    EndpointSpec(
   142	        "margin",
   143	        CATEGORY_MARKET,
   144	        (PARAM_KIND_EXCHANGE_PROBE_DATE,),
   145	        scope="exchange_aggregate",
   146	        date_bound=True,
   147	        note="margin 为交易所级融资融券汇总接口，不含个股维度；按 ts_code 所属交易所单日探测",
   148	    ),
   149	    EndpointSpec("top_list", CATEGORY_MARKET, (PARAM_KIND_PROBE_DATE_WITH_SYMBOL,), date_bound=True),
   150	    EndpointSpec("block_trade", CATEGORY_MARKET, (PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE,)),
   151	)
   152	
   153	EXCLUDED_ENDPOINTS: tuple[dict[str, str], ...] = (
   154	    {
   155	        "endpoint": "vip_*（5000 积分 VIP 全市场批量接口族）",
   156	        "classification": "unknown",
   157	        "extra_paid": "unknown",
   158	        "reason": "官方标注 5000 积分 VIP 权限，未实测，明确排除",
   159	    },
   160	    {
   161	        "endpoint": "top_inst",
   162	        "classification": "unknown",
   163	        "extra_paid": "unknown",
   164	        "reason": "龙虎榜机构明细，任务范围明确排除，未实测",
   165	    },
   166	    {
   167	        "endpoint": "实时行情类接口",
   168	        "classification": "unknown",
   169	        "extra_paid": "likely",
   170	        "reason": "实时行情属额外付费能力，未实测",
   171	    },
   172	    {
   173	        "endpoint": "历史分钟行情类接口",
   174	        "classification": "unknown",
   175	        "extra_paid": "likely",
   176	        "reason": "分钟线属额外付费能力，未实测",
   177	    },
   178	    {
   179	        "endpoint": "新闻类接口",
   180	        "classification": "unknown",
   230	        marker in lowered for marker in _RATE_LIMIT_MARKERS_EN
   231	    ):
   232	        return STATE_RATE_LIMITED
   233	    if any(marker in text for marker in _PERMISSION_MARKERS) or any(
   234	        marker in lowered for marker in _PERMISSION_MARKERS_EN
   235	    ):
   236	        return STATE_PERMISSION_DENIED
   237	    return STATE_QUERY_FAILED

   239	
   240	def is_transient_network_error(message: str) -> bool:
   241	    lowered = str(message or "").lower()
   242	    return any(marker in lowered for marker in _TRANSIENT_MARKERS)
   243	
   244	
   245	def classify_exception(exc: BaseException) -> str:
   246	    return classify_error_message(str(exc))
   247	
   248	
   249	def canonical_frame_payload(frame: pd.DataFrame | None) -> str:
   250	    if frame is None or len(frame) == 0:
   251	        return "[]"
   252	    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   253	    for row in frame.to_dict(orient="records"):
   254	        clean: dict[str, Any] = {}
   255	        for key, value in row.items():
   256	            try:
   257	                missing = bool(pd.isna(value))
   258	            except (TypeError, ValueError):
   259	                missing = False
   260	            if missing or value is None:
   261	                clean[str(key)] = None
   262	            elif isinstance(value, float) and (math.isnan(value) or math.isinf(value)):
   263	                clean[str(key)] = None
   264	            elif isinstance(value, (str, int, bool)):
   265	                clean[str(key)] = value
   266	            else:
   267	                clean[str(key)] = str(value)
   268	        records.append(clean)
   269	    return json.dumps(records, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":"))
   270	
   271	
   272	def payload_sha256(payload: str) -> str:
   273	    return hashlib.sha256(payload.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
   274	
   275	
   276	def extract_time_range(frame: pd.DataFrame | None) -> dict[str, str] | None:
   277	    if frame is None or frame.empty:
   278	        return None
   279	    for column in _DATE_RANGE_FIELD_PRIORITY:
   280	        if column not in frame.columns:
   281	            continue
   282	        values = frame[column].astype(str).str.replace(r"\D", "", regex=True)
   283	        values = values[values.str.len().eq(8)]
   284	        if not values.empty:
   285	            return {"field": column, "min": values.min(), "max": values.max()}
   286	    return None
   287	
   288	
   289	def build_params(spec: EndpointSpec, probe_context: Mapping[str, str]) -> dict[str, str]:
   290	    ts_code = probe_context["ts_code"]
   291	    params: dict[str, str] = {}
   292	    for kind in spec.param_kinds:
   293	        if kind == PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE:
   294	            params["ts_code"] = ts_code
   295	        elif kind == PARAM_KIND_STATEMENT_WINDOW:
   296	            params["start_date"] = probe_context["statement_start"]
   297	            params["end_date"] = probe_context["probe_date"]
   298	        elif kind == PARAM_KIND_RECENT_WINDOW:
   299	            params["start_date"] = probe_context["window_start"]
   300	            params["end_date"] = probe_context["probe_date"]
   301	        elif kind == PARAM_KIND_PROBE_DATE:
   302	            params["trade_date"] = probe_context["probe_date"]
   303	        elif kind == PARAM_KIND_PROBE_DATE_WITH_SYMBOL:
   304	            params["ts_code"] = ts_code
   305	            params["trade_date"] = probe_context["probe_date"]
   306	        elif kind == PARAM_KIND_EXCHANGE_PROBE_DATE:
   307	            params["trade_date"] = probe_context["probe_date"]
   308	            params["exchange"] = probe_context["exchange"]
   309	        else:
   310	            raise ValueError(f"unknown param kind: {kind}")
   311	    return params
   312	
   313	
   314	def with_fallback_date(params: Mapping[str, str], fallback_date: str) -> dict[str, str]:
   315	    updated = dict(params)
   316	    updated["trade_date"] = fallback_date
   317	    return updated
   318	
   319	
   320	def permission_status_for(state: str) -> str:
   321	    if state == STATE_PERMISSION_DENIED:
   322	        return PERMISSION_DENIED
   323	    if state in (STATE_HAS_DATA, STATE_NORMAL_NO_DATA, STATE_RATE_LIMITED):
   324	        return PERMISSION_ALLOWED
   325	    return PERMISSION_UNKNOWN
   326	
   327	
   328	def _empty_vs_error(state: str) -> str:
   329	    if state == STATE_HAS_DATA:
   330	        return "data_returned"
   331	    if state == STATE_NORMAL_NO_DATA:
   332	        return "empty_frame_without_error"
   333	    if state == STATE_QUERY_FAILED:
   334	        return "generic_error_raised"
   335	    if state == STATE_PERMISSION_DENIED:
   336	        return "permission_error_raised"
   337	    if state == STATE_RATE_LIMITED:
   338	        return "rate_limit_error_raised"
   339	    return "not_queried"
   340	
   341	
   342	def _base_record(
   343	    spec: EndpointSpec,
   344	    params: Mapping[str, str],
   345	    *,
   346	    tested_at: str,
   347	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   348	    return {
   349	        "endpoint": spec.endpoint,
   350	        "category": spec.category,
   351	        "scope": spec.scope,
   352	        "note": spec.note or None,
   353	        "params": dict(params),
   354	        "tested_at": tested_at,
   355	        "attempts": 1,
   356	        "retry_reason": None,
   357	        "first_attempt_state": None,
   358	        "state": None,
   359	        "permission_status": None,
   360	        "extra_paid": False,
   361	        "row_count": None,
   362	        "column_count": None,
   363	        "columns": None,
   364	        "time_range": None,
   365	        "response_sha256": None,
   366	        "response_sha256_scope": None,
   367	        "empty_vs_error": None,
   368	        "rate_limit": {
   369	            "per_minute": None,
   370	            "per_hour": None,
   371	            "verified_in_run": False,
   372	            "note": "单次探测无法验证限频；仅记录探测时是否触发限流错误",
   373	        },
   374	        "latency_ms": None,
   375	        "error": None,
   376	    }
   377	
   378	
   379	def _apply_frame_result(record: dict[str, Any], frame: pd.DataFrame | None) -> None:
   380	    normalized = frame if isinstance(frame, pd.DataFrame) else pd.DataFrame()
   381	    record["row_count"] = int(len(normalized))
   382	    record["column_count"] = int(normalized.shape[1]) if not normalized.empty else 0
   383	    record["columns"] = [str(column) for column in normalized.columns] if not normalized.empty else []
   384	    record["time_range"] = extract_time_range(normalized)
   385	    payload = canonical_frame_payload(normalized)
   386	    record["response_sha256"] = payload_sha256(payload)
   387	    record["response_sha256_scope"] = "frame"
   388	    record["state"] = STATE_HAS_DATA if len(normalized) else STATE_NORMAL_NO_DATA
   389	
   390	
   391	def _apply_error_result(
   392	    record: dict[str, Any],
   393	    exc: BaseException,
   394	    *,
   395	    token: str,
   396	) -> None:
   397	    state = classify_exception(exc)
   398	    record["state"] = state
   399	    record["error"] = sanitize_error_text(exc, token)
   400	    record["response_sha256"] = payload_sha256(sanitize_error_text(exc, token))
   401	    record["response_sha256_scope"] = "sanitized_error"
   402	    record["row_count"] = None
   403	    record["column_count"] = None
   404	    record["columns"] = None
   405	    record["time_range"] = None
   406	    if state == STATE_RATE_LIMITED:
   407	        record["rate_limit"]["verified_in_run"] = True
   408	
   409	
   410	def probe_endpoint(
   411	    query_fn: Callable[..., pd.DataFrame],
   412	    spec: EndpointSpec,
   413	    probe_context: Mapping[str, str],
   414	    *,
   415	    token: str = "",
   416	    now: Callable[[], datetime] | None = None,
   417	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   418	    """Execute at most one probe (plus at most one recorded retry).
   419	
   420	    Retry policy: (1) date-bound endpoints that return an empty frame on the
   421	    probe date may retry once on the fallback trading date; (2) a transient
   422	    network error may retry once with identical params.  Permission denial
   423	    and rate limiting must never be retried.
   424	    """
   425	
   426	    timestamp = (now or datetime.now)().astimezone().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   427	    params = build_params(spec, probe_context)
   428	    record = _base_record(spec, params, tested_at=timestamp)
   429	    attempt_params = params
   430	    started = datetime.now()
   431	    try:
   432	        frame = query_fn(spec.endpoint, **attempt_params)
   433	    except Exception as exc:
   434	        _apply_error_result(record, exc, token=token)
   435	        record["first_attempt_state"] = record["state"]
   436	        if record["state"] == STATE_QUERY_FAILED and is_transient_network_error(str(exc)):
   437	            retry_params = attempt_params
   438	            retry_reason = "transient_network_error_single_retry"
   439	            record["attempts"] = 2
   440	            record["retry_reason"] = retry_reason
   441	            try:
   442	                frame = query_fn(spec.endpoint, **retry_params)
   443	            except Exception as retry_exc:
   444	                _apply_error_result(record, retry_exc, token=token)
   445	            else:
   446	                _apply_frame_result(record, frame)
   447	        record["latency_ms"] = int((datetime.now() - started).total_seconds() * 1000)
   448	        record["permission_status"] = permission_status_for(record["state"])
   449	        record["empty_vs_error"] = _empty_vs_error(record["state"])
   450	        return record
   451	    _apply_frame_result(record, frame)
   452	    record["first_attempt_state"] = record["state"]
   453	    if spec.date_bound and record["state"] == STATE_NORMAL_NO_DATA:
   454	        fallback = probe_context.get("fallback_date", "")
   455	        if fallback:
   456	            record["attempts"] = 2
   457	            record["retry_reason"] = "probe_date_no_rows_retry_on_fallback_trading_date"
   458	            try:
   459	                frame = query_fn(
   460	                    spec.endpoint,
   461	                    **with_fallback_date(attempt_params, fallback),
   462	                )
   463	            except Exception as retry_exc:
   464	                _apply_error_result(record, retry_exc, token=token)
   465	            else:
   466	                _apply_frame_result(record, frame)
   467	    record["latency_ms"] = int((datetime.now() - started).total_seconds() * 1000)
   468	    record["permission_status"] = permission_status_for(record["state"])
   469	    record["empty_vs_error"] = _empty_vs_error(record["state"])
   470	    return record
   471	
   472	
   473	def not_queried_record(
   474	    spec: EndpointSpec,
   475	    probe_context: Mapping[str, str],
   476	    reason: str,
   477	    *,
   478	    now: Callable[[], datetime] | None = None,
   479	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   480	    timestamp = (now or datetime.now)().astimezone().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   481	    record = _base_record(spec, build_params(spec, probe_context), tested_at=timestamp)
   482	    record["state"] = STATE_NOT_QUERIED
   483	    record["first_attempt_state"] = STATE_NOT_QUERIED
   484	    record["permission_status"] = PERMISSION_UNKNOWN
   485	    record["empty_vs_error"] = _empty_vs_error(STATE_NOT_QUERIED)
   486	    record["error"] = sanitize_error_text(reason)
   487	    return record
   488	
   489	
   490	def build_matrix(
   490	def build_matrix(
   491	    probe_context: Mapping[str, str],
   492	    records: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   493	    *,
   494	    token_status: str,
   495	    token_source: str = "",
   496	    token_env_override_applied: bool | None = None,
   497	    generated_at: str | None = None,
   498	    excluded: Sequence[Mapping[str, str]] = EXCLUDED_ENDPOINTS,
   499	    state_evidence: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]] | None = None,
   500	    notes: Sequence[str] = (),
   501	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   502	    by_state: dict[str, int] = {}
   503	    by_permission: dict[str, int] = {}
   504	    for record in records:
   505	        by_state[record["state"]] = by_state.get(record["state"], 0) + 1
   506	        by_permission[record["permission_status"]] = (
   507	            by_permission.get(record["permission_status"], 0) + 1
   508	        )
   509	    evidence: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {
   510	        state: {
   511	            "fixture": None,
   512	            "observed_in_real_run": False,
   513	            "real_run_examples": [],
   514	        }
   515	        for state in (
   516	            STATE_HAS_DATA,
   517	            STATE_NORMAL_NO_DATA,
   518	            STATE_QUERY_FAILED,
   519	            STATE_PERMISSION_DENIED,
   520	            STATE_RATE_LIMITED,
   521	            STATE_NOT_QUERIED,
   522	        )
   523	    }
   524	    for state, info in (state_evidence or {}).items():
   525	        if state in evidence:
   526	            evidence[state].update(info)
   527	    for record in records:
   528	        state = record["state"]
   529	        if state in evidence and not evidence[state]["observed_in_real_run"]:
   530	            evidence[state]["observed_in_real_run"] = True
   531	        if state in evidence and record["endpoint"] not in evidence[state]["real_run_examples"]:
   532	            evidence[state]["real_run_examples"].append(record["endpoint"])
   533	    for state in evidence:
   534	        if not evidence[state]["observed_in_real_run"]:
   535	            evidence[state]["real_run_examples"] = []
   536	    return {
   537	        "schema_version": MATRIX_SCHEMA_VERSION,
   538	        "task_id": TASK_ID,
   539	        "generated_at": generated_at
   540	        or datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
   541	        "token_status": token_status,
   542	        "token_source": token_source or None,
   543	        "token_env_override_applied": token_env_override_applied,
   544	        "probe": {
   545	            "symbol": probe_context["ts_code"],
   546	            "exchange": probe_context.get("exchange"),
   547	            "probe_date": probe_context["probe_date"],
   548	            "fallback_date": probe_context.get("fallback_date"),
   549	            "window_start": probe_context.get("window_start"),
   550	            "statement_start": probe_context.get("statement_start"),
   551	            "policy": (
   552	                "每个 endpoint 最多一次正常探测；仅瞬态网络错误或日期型空结果允许一次带原因记录的重试；"
   553	                "权限拒绝与限流绝不重试；探测间 sleep 限速"
   554	            ),
   555	        },
   556	        "summary": {
   557	            "probed_endpoints": len(records),
   558	            "by_state": by_state,
   559	            "by_permission": by_permission,
   560	        },
   561	        "endpoints": [dict(record) for record in records],
   562	        "excluded": [dict(item) for item in excluded],
   563	        "state_evidence": evidence,
   564	        "notes": [
   565	            "矩阵结果只来自本次真实探测，不引用静态 SOURCE_CAPABILITY_MATRIX，也不按积分等级推测",
   566	            "矩阵不包含真实响应正文、Token、Cookie 或 Authorization；错误文本经脱敏清洗",
   567	            "原始响应以结构化元数据（行/列数、列名、日期范围）与 SHA-256 摘要形式留存",
   568	            *notes,
   569	        ],
   570	    }
   571	
   572	
   573	_CATEGORY_LABELS = {
   574	    CATEGORY_FINANCIAL: "财务",
   575	    CATEGORY_GOVERNANCE: "治理事件",
   576	    CATEGORY_MARKET: "市场行为",
   577	}
   578	
   579	
   580	def _md_escape(value: Any) -> str:
   581	    text = "" if value is None else str(value)
   582	    return text.replace("|", "\\|").replace("\n", " ")
   583	
   584	
   585	def annotate_row_cap_hits(matrix: Mapping[str, Any], *, cap: int = ROW_CAP_DEFAULT) -> None:
   586	    """Flag any record that reached the upstream row cap.
   587	
   588	    Scope-agnostic on purpose (001A-R1): even a symbol-scoped probe can hit
   589	    the cap when upstream ignores filter params, and that must stay visible.
   590	    """
   591	
   592	    for record in matrix["endpoints"]:
   593	        if record.get("row_count") is not None and record["row_count"] >= cap:
   594	            record["row_cap_suspected"] = True
   595	    if any(record.get("row_cap_suspected") for record in matrix["endpoints"]):
   596	        notes = matrix["notes"]
   597	        note = _ROW_CAP_NOTE.format(cap=cap)
   598	        if note not in notes:
   599	            notes.append(note)
   600	
   601	
   602	def render_markdown(matrix: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
   603	    probe = matrix["probe"]
   604	    summary = matrix["summary"]
   605	    lines: list[str] = []
   606	    lines.append(f"# Tushare 2000 积分真实权限矩阵（{matrix['task_id']}）")
   607	    lines.append("")
   608	    lines.append(f"- 生成时间：{matrix['generated_at']}")
   609	    lines.append(f"- Token 状态：`{matrix['token_status']}`（不记录 Token 任何明文信息）")
   610	    token_source = matrix.get("token_source")
   611	    if token_source:
   612	        source_label = (
   613	            "git-ignored .env 文件"
   614	            if token_source == TOKEN_SOURCE_DOTENV
   615	            else "继承环境变量"
   616	        )
   617	        source_line = f"- Token 来源：`{token_source}`（{source_label}）"
   618	        if matrix.get("token_env_override_applied"):
   619	            source_line += "；继承环境变量中的 TUSHARE_TOKEN 已被显式 .env 值覆盖，矩阵归属以 .env 为准"
   620	        lines.append(source_line)
   621	    lines.append(
   622	        f"- 探测标的：`{probe['symbol']}`（{probe['exchange']}），"
   623	        f"探测日 `{probe['probe_date']}`，回退日 `{probe['fallback_date']}`"
   624	    )
   625	    lines.append(f"- 探测策略：{probe['policy']}")
   626	    lines.append("")
   627	    lines.append("## 汇总")
   628	    lines.append("")
   629	    lines.append(f"- 实测 endpoint 数：{summary['probed_endpoints']}")
   630	    for state, count in sorted(summary["by_state"].items()):
   631	        lines.append(f"- `{state}`：{count}")
   632	    for status, count in sorted(summary["by_permission"].items()):
   633	        lines.append(f"- 权限 `{status}`：{count}")
   634	    lines.append("")
   635	    lines.append("## 实测权限矩阵")
   636	    lines.append("")
   637	    lines.append(
   638	        "| endpoint | 类别 | 状态 | 权限 | 行数 | 数据时间范围 | 额外付费 | 重试 | SHA-256(前12位) |"
   639	    )
   640	    lines.append("|---|---|---|---|---:|---|---|---|---|")
   641	    for record in matrix["endpoints"]:
   642	        digest = record.get("response_sha256") or ""
   643	        time_range = record.get("time_range")
   644	        time_range_text = (
   645	            f"{time_range['min']}~{time_range['max']}({time_range['field']})"
   646	            if time_range
   647	            else "-"
   648	        )
   649	        retry = "是" if record.get("attempts", 1) > 1 else "否"
   650	        lines.append(
   651	            "| {endpoint} | {category} | `{state}` | {permission} | {rows} | {time_range} "
   652	            "| {extra_paid} | {retry} | `{digest}` |".format(
   653	                endpoint=_md_escape(record["endpoint"]),
   654	                category=_CATEGORY_LABELS.get(record["category"], record["category"]),
   655	                state=_md_escape(record["state"]),
   656	                permission=_md_escape(record["permission_status"]),
   657	                rows=record.get("row_count") if record.get("row_count") is not None else "-",
   658	                time_range=_md_escape(time_range_text),
   659	                extra_paid="是" if record.get("extra_paid") else "否",
   660	                retry=retry,
   661	                digest=digest[:12] if digest else "-",
   662	            )
   663	        )
   664	    lines.append("")

 # 修改日志
 
+## 2026-08-16 | TA-TUSHARE-2000-001A-R1：权限矩阵补修与真实重跑（P0）
+
+- 修复 c73ed4f Codex review（gpt-5.6-sol high）3×P1+2×P2：
+  - **P1 Token 归属**：`scripts/audit_tushare_capability.py` 不再用
+    `load_dotenv(override=False)`（继承环境变量可静默压过指定 `.env`）。新增
+    `resolve_token()` 显式解析并记录来源：`.env` 恒优先于继承环境变量，返回
+    `(token, dotenv_file/inherited_environment, env_override_applied)`；矩阵新增
+    `token_source`/`token_env_override_applied` 字段（schema 1.0→1.1），Markdown、
+    receipt 与 dry-run 输出均展示来源标识；新增 `--dotenv` 参数支持显式指定文件。
+  - **P1 repurchase 全市场抓取**：`ENDPOINT_SPECS` 中 repurchase 由公告日窗口
+    （scope=market_window_filtered，实测命中 2000 行上限）改为单 `ts_code` 最小查询
+    （scope=symbol）。真实重跑验证上游接受 `ts_code`：2000 行→6 行（603629.SH，
+    公告日 20240207~20260326），receipt 新增“repurchase 单股查询记录”章节。
+  - **P1 泄漏扫描不阻断**：`write_outputs` 改为先组装 JSON/Markdown/receipt 全文，
+    `scan_artifacts_for_secret()` 命中即**拒绝写盘**并由 `main` 以退出码 3 fail
+    closed（0 成功 / 2 NO_KEY / 3 泄漏）。
+  - **P2 北交所映射**：`exchange_for_ts_code` 改为 SH→SSE、SZ→SZSE、BJ→BJSE，
+    未知后缀抛 ValueError fail closed（原逻辑 `.BJ` 误判 SSE）。
+  - **P2 自定义输出目录崩溃**：`relative_to(ROOT)` 改为 `display_path()`，root 外
+    路径回退绝对路径字符串，不再抛异常。
+- 附带加固：`annotate_row_cap_hits` 不再限定 market_window_filtered scope——任意
+  endpoint 命中行数上限都标注 `row_cap_suspected`（防止上游忽略过滤参数时失明）。

+  （HAS_DATA 22 + NORMAL_NO_DATA 2：express 无快报、top_list 非上榜日）；与旧矩阵

   211	def sanitize_error_text(text: Any, *secrets: str) -> str:
   212	    """Return a credential-free error excerpt safe for committed artifacts."""
   213	
   214	    message = str(text or "").strip()
   215	    for secret in secrets:
   216	        cleaned = str(secret or "").strip()
   217	        if cleaned:
   218	            message = message.replace(cleaned, "[REDACTED]")
   219	    message = _CREDENTIAL_KEY_RE.sub(
   220	        lambda match: f"{match.group(1)}{match.group(2)}[REDACTED]", message
   221	    )
   222	    message = _OPAQUE_BLOB_RE.sub("[REDACTED]", message)
   223	    return message[:300]
   224	
   225	
   226	def classify_error_message(message: str) -> str:
   227	    text = str(message or "")
   228	    lowered = text.lower()
   229	    if any(marker in text for marker in _RATE_LIMIT_MARKERS) or any(
   230	        marker in lowered for marker in _RATE_LIMIT_MARKERS_EN
   231	    ):
   232	        return STATE_RATE_LIMITED
   233	    if any(marker in text for marker in _PERMISSION_MARKERS) or any(
   234	        marker in lowered for marker in _PERMISSION_MARKERS_EN
   235	    ):
   236	        return STATE_PERMISSION_DENIED
   237	    return STATE_QUERY_FAILED
   238	
   239	
   240	def is_transient_network_error(message: str) -> bool:
   241	    lowered = str(message or "").lower()
   242	    return any(marker in lowered for marker in _TRANSIENT_MARKERS)
   243	
   244	
   245	def classify_exception(exc: BaseException) -> str:
   246	    return classify_error_message(str(exc))
   247	
   248	
   249	def canonical_frame_payload(frame: pd.DataFrame | None) -> str:
   250	    if frame is None or len(frame) == 0:
   251	        return "[]"
   252	    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   253	    for row in frame.to_dict(orient="records"):
   254	        clean: dict[str, Any] = {}
   255	        for key, value in row.items():
   256	            try:
   257	                missing = bool(pd.isna(value))
   258	            except (TypeError, ValueError):
   259	                missing = False
   260	            if missing or value is None:
   261	                clean[str(key)] = None
   262	            elif isinstance(value, float) and (math.isnan(value) or math.isinf(value)):
   263	                clean[str(key)] = None
   264	            elif isinstance(value, (str, int, bool)):
   265	                clean[str(key)] = value
   266	            else:
   267	                clean[str(key)] = str(value)
   268	        records.append(clean)
   269	    return json.dumps(records, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":"))
   270	
   271	
   272	def payload_sha256(payload: str) -> str:
   273	    return hashlib.sha256(payload.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
   274	
   275	
   276	def extract_time_range(frame: pd.DataFrame | None) -> dict[str, str] | None:
   277	    if frame is None or frame.empty:
   278	        return None
   279	    for column in _DATE_RANGE_FIELD_PRIORITY:
   280	        if column not in frame.columns:
   281	            continue
   282	        values = frame[column].astype(str).str.replace(r"\D", "", regex=True)
   283	        values = values[values.str.len().eq(8)]
   284	        if not values.empty:
   285	            return {"field": column, "min": values.min(), "max": values.max()}
   286	    return None
   287	
   288	
   289	def build_params(spec: EndpointSpec, probe_context: Mapping[str, str]) -> dict[str, str]:
   290	    ts_code = probe_context["ts_code"]
   291	    params: dict[str, str] = {}
   292	    for kind in spec.param_kinds:
   293	        if kind == PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE:
   294	            params["ts_code"] = ts_code
   295	        elif kind == PARAM_KIND_STATEMENT_WINDOW:
   296	            params["start_date"] = probe_context["statement_start"]
   297	            params["end_date"] = probe_context["probe_date"]
   298	        elif kind == PARAM_KIND_RECENT_WINDOW:
   299	            params["start_date"] = probe_context["window_start"]
   300	            params["end_date"] = probe_context["probe_date"]
   301	        elif kind == PARAM_KIND_PROBE_DATE:
   302	            params["trade_date"] = probe_context["probe_date"]
   303	        elif kind == PARAM_KIND_PROBE_DATE_WITH_SYMBOL:
   304	            params["ts_code"] = ts_code
   305	            params["trade_date"] = probe_context["probe_date"]
   306	        elif kind == PARAM_KIND_EXCHANGE_PROBE_DATE:
   307	            params["trade_date"] = probe_context["probe_date"]
   308	            params["exchange"] = probe_context["exchange"]
   309	        else:
   310	            raise ValueError(f"unknown param kind: {kind}")
   311	    return params
   312	
   313	
   314	def with_fallback_date(params: Mapping[str, str], fallback_date: str) -> dict[str, str]:
   315	    updated = dict(params)
   316	    updated["trade_date"] = fallback_date
   317	    return updated
   318	
   319	
   320	def permission_status_for(state: str) -> str:
   321	    if state == STATE_PERMISSION_DENIED:
   322	        return PERMISSION_DENIED
   323	    if state in (STATE_HAS_DATA, STATE_NORMAL_NO_DATA, STATE_RATE_LIMITED):
   324	        return PERMISSION_ALLOWED
   325	    return PERMISSION_UNKNOWN
   326	
   327	
   328	def _empty_vs_error(state: str) -> str:
   329	    if state == STATE_HAS_DATA:
   330	        return "data_returned"
   331	    if state == STATE_NORMAL_NO_DATA:
   332	        return "empty_frame_without_error"
   333	    if state == STATE_QUERY_FAILED:
   334	        return "generic_error_raised"
   335	    if state == STATE_PERMISSION_DENIED:
   336	        return "permission_error_raised"
   337	    if state == STATE_RATE_LIMITED:
   338	        return "rate_limit_error_raised"
   339	    return "not_queried"
   340	
   341	
   342	def _base_record(
   343	    spec: EndpointSpec,
   344	    params: Mapping[str, str],
   345	    *,
   346	    tested_at: str,
   347	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   348	    return {
   349	        "endpoint": spec.endpoint,
   350	        "category": spec.category,
   351	        "scope": spec.scope,
   352	        "note": spec.note or None,
   353	        "params": dict(params),
   354	        "tested_at": tested_at,
   355	        "attempts": 1,
   356	        "retry_reason": None,
   357	        "first_attempt_state": None,
   358	        "state": None,
   359	        "permission_status": None,
   360	        "extra_paid": False,
   361	        "row_count": None,
   362	        "column_count": None,
   363	        "columns": None,
   364	        "time_range": None,
   365	        "response_sha256": None,
   366	        "response_sha256_scope": None,
   367	        "empty_vs_error": None,
   368	        "rate_limit": {
   369	            "per_minute": None,
   370	            "per_hour": None,
   371	            "verified_in_run": False,
   372	            "note": "单次探测无法验证限频；仅记录探测时是否触发限流错误",
   373	        },
   374	        "latency_ms": None,
   375	        "error": None,
   376	    }
   377	
   378	
   379	def _apply_frame_result(record: dict[str, Any], frame: pd.DataFrame | None) -> None:
   380	    normalized = frame if isinstance(frame, pd.DataFrame) else pd.DataFrame()
   381	    record["row_count"] = int(len(normalized))
   382	    record["column_count"] = int(normalized.shape[1]) if not normalized.empty else 0
   383	    record["columns"] = [str(column) for column in normalized.columns] if not normalized.empty else []
   384	    record["time_range"] = extract_time_range(normalized)
   385	    payload = canonical_frame_payload(normalized)
   386	    record["response_sha256"] = payload_sha256(payload)
   387	    record["response_sha256_scope"] = "frame"
   388	    record["state"] = STATE_HAS_DATA if len(normalized) else STATE_NORMAL_NO_DATA
   389

--- base version audit script for comparison ---
     1	"""TA-TUSHARE-2000-001A: real Tushare 2000-point permission matrix audit.
     2	
     3	Runs one minimal, single-stock, low-frequency probe per endpoint listed in
     4	the task scope and writes a sanitized permission matrix (JSON + Markdown)
     5	plus a run receipt into a ``docs/task_runs/TA-TUSHARE-2000-001A-*``
     6	archive.  The Tushare token is read exclusively from the git-ignored
     7	``.env`` file; every artifact only records ``HAS_KEY``/``NO_KEY``.
     8	"""

    44	DEFAULT_STATEMENT_START = "20250101"
    45	FIXTURES_DIR = ROOT / "tests" / "fixtures" / "tushare_capability"
    46	MATRIX_JSON_NAME = "tushare_permission_matrix.json"
    47	MATRIX_MD_NAME = "tushare_permission_matrix.md"
    48	RECEIPT_NAME = "run-receipt.md"
    49	
    50	
    51	def exchange_for_ts_code(ts_code: str) -> str:
    52	    suffix = ts_code.rsplit(".", 1)[-1].upper()
    53	    if suffix == "SZ":
    54	        return "SZSE"
    55	    return "SSE"
    56	
    57	
    58	def normalize_symbol(symbol: str) -> str:
    59	    from tradingagents.dataflows.providers.cn_tushare_provider import _normalize_ts_code
    60	
    61	    return _normalize_ts_code(symbol)
    62	
    63	
    64	def build_probe_context(args: argparse.Namespace) -> dict[str, str]:
    65	    ts_code = normalize_symbol(args.symbol)
    66	    return {
    67	        "ts_code": ts_code,
    68	        "exchange": exchange_for_ts_code(ts_code),
    69	        "probe_date": args.probe_date,
    70	        "fallback_date": args.fallback_date,
    71	        "window_start": args.window_start,
    72	        "statement_start": args.statement_start,
    73	    }
    74	
    75	
    76	def state_evidence_from_fixtures() -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    77	    evidence: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    78	    if not FIXTURES_DIR.is_dir():
    79	        return evidence
    80	    for path in sorted(FIXTURES_DIR.glob("*.json")):
    81	        try:
    82	            payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    83	        except (OSError, ValueError):
    84	            continue
    85	        state = payload.get("expected_state")
    86	        if state:
    87	            evidence[str(state)] = {
    88	                "fixture": f"tests/fixtures/tushare_capability/{path.name}",
    89	                "fixture_kind": payload.get("fixture_kind", "sanitized"),
    90	            }
    91	    return evidence
    92	
    93	
    94	def make_query_fn(token: str):
    95	    import tushare as ts
    96	
    97	    client = ts.pro_api(token)
    98	
    99	    def query(endpoint: str, **kwargs: Any):
   100	        return client.query(endpoint, **kwargs)
   101	
   102	    return query
   103	
   104	
   105	def scan_text_for_secret(text: str, token: str) -> bool:
   106	    if token and token in text:
   107	        return True
   108	    lowered = text.lower()
   109	    return "authorization:" in lowered or "cookie:" in lowered
   110	
   111	
   112	def run_probes(query_fn, probe_context: Mapping[str, str], *, token: str, sleep_seconds: float):
   113	    records = []
   114	    for index, spec in enumerate(ENDPOINT_SPECS):
   115	        if index:
   116	            time.sleep(sleep_seconds)
   117	        try:
   118	            record = probe_endpoint(query_fn, spec, probe_context, token=token)
   119	        except Exception as exc:
   120	            record = not_queried_record(
   121	                spec,
   122	                probe_context,
   123	                f"probe runner unexpected failure: {sanitize_error_text(exc, token)}",
   124	            )
   125	        records.append(record)
   126	        print(
   127	            f"[probe] {record['endpoint']:<20} {record['state']:<18} "
   128	            f"rows={record.get('row_count') if record.get('row_count') is not None else '-'} "
   129	            f"attempts={record.get('attempts', 1)}",
   130	            flush=True,
   131	        )
   132	    return records
   133	
   134	
   135	def write_outputs(matrix: dict[str, Any], output_dir: Path, *, token: str) -> list[Path]:
   136	    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   137	    json_path = output_dir / MATRIX_JSON_NAME
   138	    md_path = output_dir / MATRIX_MD_NAME
   139	    receipt_path = output_dir / RECEIPT_NAME
   140	    json_path.write_text(
   141	        json.dumps(matrix, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=False) + "\n",
   142	        encoding="utf-8",
   143	    )
   144	    markdown = render_markdown(matrix)
   145	    md_path.write_text(markdown, encoding="utf-8")
   146	    scanned = [json_path, md_path]
   147	    clean = not any(scan_text_for_secret(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), token) for path in scanned)
   148	    retry_lines = [
   149	        f"- `{record['endpoint']}`：attempts={record['attempts']}，reason=`{record['retry_reason']}`"
   150	        for record in matrix["endpoints"]
   151	        if record.get("attempts", 1) > 1
   152	    ]
   153	    receipt = [
   154	        f"# Run receipt — {TASK_ID}",
   155	        "",
   156	        f"- 运行时间：{matrix['generated_at']}",
   157	        f"- Token 来源：git-ignored `.env`（TUSHARE_TOKEN），状态 `{matrix['token_status']}`，"
   158	        "任何产物不包含 Token 明文",
   159	        f"- 探测标的：`{matrix['probe']['symbol']}`；探测日 `{matrix['probe']['probe_date']}`；"
   160	        f"回退日 `{matrix['probe']['fallback_date']}`",
   161	        f"- 实测 endpoint 数：{matrix['summary']['probed_endpoints']}；"
   162	        f"状态分布：{json.dumps(matrix['summary']['by_state'], ensure_ascii=False)}",
   163	        "",
   164	        "## 重试记录",
   165	        "",
   166	        *(retry_lines or ["- 无重试"]),
   167	        "",
   168	        "## 凭据自检",
   169	        "",
   170	        f"- 对 {MATRIX_JSON_NAME} 与 {MATRIX_MD_NAME} 扫描 Token/Authorization/Cookie："
   171	        f"{'clean' if clean else 'LEAK DETECTED'}",
   172	        "- 本回执与矩阵均不含真实响应正文，只含结构化元数据与 SHA-256 摘要",
   173	        "",
   174	    ]
   175	    receipt_path.write_text("\n".join(receipt), encoding="utf-8")
   176	    return [*scanned, receipt_path]
   177	
   178	
   179	def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
   180	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Tushare 2000-point real permission matrix audit")
   181	    parser.add_argument("--symbol", default=DEFAULT_SYMBOL)
   182	    parser.add_argument("--probe-date", default=DEFAULT_PROBE_DATE)
   183	    parser.add_argument("--fallback-date", default=DEFAULT_FALLBACK_DATE)
   184	    parser.add_argument("--window-start", default=DEFAULT_WINDOW_START)
   185	    parser.add_argument("--statement-start", default=DEFAULT_STATEMENT_START)
   186	    parser.add_argument("--sleep-seconds", type=float, default=0.6)
   187	    parser.add_argument(
   188	        "--output-dir",
   189	        default=None,
   190	        help="default: docs/task_runs/TA-TUSHARE-2000-001A-<YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS>",
   191	    )
   192	    parser.add_argument(
   193	        "--no-dotenv",
   194	        action="store_true",
   195	        help="跳过 .env 加载（仅供测试隔离使用；Token 只能来自环境）",
   196	    )
   197	    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="打印探测计划，不发起网络请求")
   198	    return parser
   199	
   200	
   201	def main(
   202	    argv: list[str] | None = None,
   203	    *,
   204	    environ: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,
   205	    dotenv_path: Path | None = None,
   206	) -> int:
   207	    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
   208	    if not args.no_dotenv:
   209	        load_dotenv(dotenv_path if dotenv_path is not None else ROOT / ".env", override=False)
   210	    import os
   211	
   212	    env = os.environ if environ is None else environ
   213	    token = str(env.get("TUSHARE_TOKEN", "") or "").strip()
   214	    token_status = TOKEN_STATUS_HAS_KEY if token else TOKEN_STATUS_NO_KEY
   215	    probe_context = build_probe_context(args)
   216	    if token_status == TOKEN_STATUS_NO_KEY:
   217	        print("[audit] TUSHARE_TOKEN NO_KEY：拒绝探测（fail closed），Token 只允许来自 git-ignored .env")
   218	        return 2
   219	    if args.dry_run:
   220	        print(f"[audit] token={token_status} symbol={probe_context['ts_code']} probe_date={args.probe_date}")
   221	        for spec in ENDPOINT_SPECS:
   222	            print(f"[dry-run] {spec.endpoint:<20} category={spec.category:<10} kinds={spec.param_kinds}")
   223	        print(f"[dry-run] excluded items: {len(EXCLUDED_ENDPOINTS)}")
   224	        return 0
   225	    started = datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   226	    stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
   227	    output_dir = (
   228	        Path(args.output_dir)
   229	        if args.output_dir
   230	        else ROOT / "docs" / "task_runs" / f"{TASK_ID}-{stamp}"
   231	    )
   232	    print(
   233	        f"[audit] token={token_status} symbol={probe_context['ts_code']} "
   234	        f"probe_date={args.probe_date} output={output_dir}"
   235	    )
   236	    query_fn = make_query_fn(token)
   237	    records = run_probes(query_fn, probe_context, token=token, sleep_seconds=args.sleep_seconds)
   238	    matrix = build_matrix(
   239	        probe_context,
   240	        records,
   241	        token_status=token_status,
   242	        generated_at=started,
   243	        state_evidence=state_evidence_from_fixtures(),
   244	        notes=[
   245	            f"探测脚本：scripts/audit_tushare_capability.py，探测间隔 {args.sleep_seconds}s",
   246	        ],
   247	    )
   248	    annotate_row_cap_hits(matrix)
   249	    written = write_outputs(matrix, output_dir, token=token)
   250	    print(f"[audit] wrote {', '.join(str(path.relative_to(ROOT)) for path in written)}")
   251	    return 0
   252	
   253	
   254	if __name__ == "__main__":
   255	    raise SystemExit(main())

    71	    while cursor <= end_date:
    72	        window_end = min(cursor + timedelta(days=max_days - 1), end_date)
    73	        windows.append((cursor.strftime("%Y%m%d"), window_end.strftime("%Y%m%d")))
    74	        cursor = window_end + timedelta(days=1)
