    77	
    78	def _normalize_ts_code(symbol: str) -> str:
    79	    text = str(symbol or "").strip().upper()
    80	    match = re.fullmatch(r"(\d{6})(?:\.(SH|SZ|BJ))?", text)
    81	    if not match:
    82	        raise ValueError(f"invalid A-share symbol: {symbol!r}")
    83	    code, suffix = match.groups()
    84	    expected_suffix = None
    85	    if code.startswith(("60", "68", "90")):
    86	        expected_suffix = "SH"
    87	    elif code.startswith(("00", "30", "20")):
    88	        expected_suffix = "SZ"
    89	    elif code.startswith(("43", "82", "83", "87", "88", "92")):
    90	        expected_suffix = "BJ"
    91	    if expected_suffix is None:
    92	        raise ValueError(f"cannot infer exchange for A-share symbol: {symbol!r}")
    93	    if suffix is not None and suffix != expected_suffix:
    94	        raise ValueError(
    95	            f"exchange suffix {suffix} conflicts with A-share code {code}"
    96	        )
    97	    suffix = expected_suffix
    98	    return f"{code}.{suffix}"
    99	
   100	
   101	def _present(value: Any) -> bool:
   102	    if value is None:
   103	        return False
   104	    try:
   105	        if bool(pd.isna(value)):
   106	            return False
   107	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   108	        pass
   109	    text = str(value).strip().lower()
   110	    return bool(text and text not in {"nan", "none", "null", "-", "--", "—"})
   111

   113	def _safe_error(exc: Exception, *secrets: str) -> str:
   114	    message = str(exc)
   115	    candidates = {os.getenv("TUSHARE_TOKEN", "").strip()}
   116	    candidates.update(str(secret or "").strip() for secret in secrets)
   117	    for secret in candidates:
   118	        if secret:
   119	            message = message.replace(secret, "[REDACTED]")
   120	    return f"{type(exc).__name__}: {message[:300]}"

    55	# this keeps TA read-only with respect to those repositories.
    56	_FORBIDDEN_PATH_SEGMENTS = (
    57	    "tree_work",
    58	    "knowledge",
    59	    "knowledge_base",

    69	def _write_json_immutable(path: Path, payload: object) -> None:
    70	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    71	    file_descriptor, temporary_name = tempfile.mkstemp(
    72	        prefix=f".{path.name}.", suffix=".tmp", dir=path.parent
    73	    )
    74	    temporary_path = Path(temporary_name)
    75	    try:
    76	        with os.fdopen(file_descriptor, "w", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
    77	            json.dump(payload, handle, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    78	            handle.write("\n")
    79	            handle.flush()
    80	            os.fsync(handle.fileno())
    81	        try:
    82	            os.link(temporary_path, path)
    83	        except FileExistsError as exc:
    84	            raise FileExistsError(
    85	                f"refusing to overwrite existing governance pack: {path}"
    86	            ) from exc
    87	        directory_fd = os.open(path.parent, os.O_RDONLY)
    88	        try:
    89	            os.fsync(directory_fd)
    90	        finally:
    91	            os.close(directory_fd)
    92	    finally:
    93	        temporary_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    94	
    95	
    96	def guard_output_path(path: Path) -> None:
    97	    if path.exists():
    98	        raise FileExistsError(f"refusing to overwrite existing output: {path}")
    99	    resolved = str(path.resolve()).lower()
   100	    segments = {part.strip() for part in resolved.split("/") if part.strip()}
   101	    for forbidden in _FORBIDDEN_PATH_SEGMENTS:
   102	        if forbidden in segments:
   103	            raise ValueError(
   104	                "governance export must not write into knowledge-base or "
   105	                f"HaiRui-like directories (matched segment {forbidden!r}): {path}"
   106	            )
   107

   109	def make_query_fn(token: str):
   110	    import tushare as ts
   111	
   112	    client = ts.pro_api(token)
   113	
   114	    def query(endpoint: str, **kwargs: Any):
   115	        return client.query(endpoint, **kwargs)
   116	
   117	    return query
   118	
   119	
   120	def make_fixture_query_fn(fixture_path: Path):
   121	    payload = json.loads(fixture_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   122	    responses = payload.get("endpoints") if isinstance(payload, dict) else None
   123	    if not isinstance(responses, dict):
   124	        raise ValueError(f"fixture must contain an 'endpoints' mapping: {fixture_path}")
   125	
   126	    def query(endpoint: str, **_kwargs: Any):
   127	        rows = responses.get(endpoint)
   128	        if rows is None:
   129	            return pd.DataFrame()
   130	        if isinstance(rows, dict) and "error" in rows:
   131	            raise RuntimeError(rows["error"])
   132	        return pd.DataFrame(rows)
   133	
   134	    return query
   135	
   136	
   137	def load_endpoint_permissions(matrix_path: Path | None):
   138	    if matrix_path is None or not Path(matrix_path).exists():
   139	        return {}, None, None
   140	    try:
   141	        matrix = json.loads(Path(matrix_path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   142	    except (OSError, ValueError):
   143	        return {}, None, None
   144	    permissions = {
   145	        str(record.get("endpoint")): str(record.get("permission_status"))
   146	        for record in matrix.get("endpoints", [])
   147	        if record.get("endpoint") and record.get("permission_status")
   148	    }
   149	    tier = matrix.get("tier") or "2000_points"
   150	    try:
   151	        ref = str(Path(matrix_path).relative_to(ROOT))
   152	    except ValueError:
   153	        ref = str(matrix_path)
   154	    return permissions, tier, ref

   157	def sanitize_summary(pack: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   158	    return {
   159	        "schema": pack["schema"],
   160	        "governance_schema_version": pack["governance_schema_version"],
   161	        "symbol": pack["symbol"],
   162	        "as_of": pack["as_of"],

   171	        "endpoints": {
   172	            endpoint: {
   173	                "state": record["state"],
   174	                "row_count": record["row_count"],
   175	                "eligible_row_count": record["eligible_row_count"],
   176	                "data_period": record["data_period"],
   177	                "response_sha256": record["response_sha256"],
   178	                "source_endpoint": record["source_endpoint"],
   179	                "permission_status": record["permission_status"],
   180	                "error": record["error"],
   250	
   251	    env = os.environ if environ is None else environ
   252	    token = str(env.get("TUSHARE_TOKEN", "") or "").strip()
   253	    token_status = TOKEN_STATUS_HAS_KEY if token else TOKEN_STATUS_NO_KEY
   254	
   255	    if args.fixture is not None:
   256	        query_fn = make_fixture_query_fn(args.fixture)
   257	        print(f"[governance] fixture mode token={token_status} fixture={args.fixture}")
   258	    elif token_status == TOKEN_STATUS_NO_KEY:
   259	        print(
   260	            "[governance] TUSHARE_TOKEN NO_KEY：fail closed（离线请用 --fixture），"
   261	            "Token 只允许来自 git-ignored .env"
   262	        )
   263	        return 2
   264	    else:
   265	        query_fn = make_query_fn(token)
   266	
   267	    permissions, tier, matrix_ref = load_endpoint_permissions(args.permission_matrix)
   268	    print(
   269	        f"[governance] token={token_status} symbol={args.symbol} "
   270	        f"as_of={args.as_of} output={args.output}"
   271	    )
   272	    started = datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   273	
   274	    def throttled_query(endpoint: str, **kwargs: Any):
   275	        time.sleep(args.sleep_seconds)
   276	        return query_fn(endpoint, **kwargs)
   277	
   278	    try:
   279	        pack = build_governance_event_pack(
   280	            symbol=args.symbol,
   281	            as_of=args.as_of,
   282	            query_fn=throttled_query,
   283	            token=token,
   284	            token_status=token_status,
   285	            collection_mode="fixture" if args.fixture is not None else "live",
   286	            permission_tier=tier,
   287	            permission_matrix_ref=matrix_ref,
   288	            endpoint_permissions=permissions,
   289	            generated_at=started,
   290	        )
   291	    except ValueError as exc:
   292	        print(f"[governance] invalid request or pack: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
   293	        return 2
   294	
   295	    validate_governance_pack(pack)
   296	    dumped = json.dumps(pack, ensure_ascii=False)
   297	    if scan_text_for_secret(dumped, token):
   298	        print("[governance] credential self-check FAILED: refusing to write", file=sys.stderr)
   299	        return 3
   300	    try:
   301	        _write_json_immutable(args.output, pack)
   302	    except FileExistsError as exc:
   303	        print(f"[governance] {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
   304	        return 2
   305	    print(json.dumps(sanitize_summary(pack), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
   306	    return 0 if pack["status"] == "HAS_DATA" else 2
   307	
   308	
   309	if __name__ == "__main__":
   310	    raise SystemExit(main())

    53	def _write_json_immutable(path: Path, payload: object) -> None:
    54	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    55	    file_descriptor, temporary_name = tempfile.mkstemp(
    56	        prefix=f".{path.name}.", suffix=".tmp", dir=path.parent
    57	    )
    58	    temporary_path = Path(temporary_name)
    59	    try:
    60	        with os.fdopen(file_descriptor, "w", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
    61	            json.dump(payload, handle, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    62	            handle.write("\n")
    63	            handle.flush()
    64	            os.fsync(handle.fileno())
    65	        try:
    66	            os.link(temporary_path, path)
    67	        except FileExistsError as exc:
    68	            raise FileExistsError(
    69	                f"refusing to overwrite existing evidence pack: {path}"
    70	            ) from exc
    71	        directory_fd = os.open(path.parent, os.O_RDONLY)
    72	        try:
    73	            os.fsync(directory_fd)
    74	        finally:
    75	            os.close(directory_fd)
    76	    finally:
    77	        temporary_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    78	
    79	
    80	def make_query_fn(token: str):
    81	    import tushare as ts
    82	
    83	    client = ts.pro_api(token)
    84	
    85	    def query(endpoint: str, **kwargs: Any):
    86	        return client.query(endpoint, **kwargs)
    87	
    88	    return query
    89	
    90	
    91	def make_fixture_query_fn(fixture_path: Path):
    92	    payload = json.loads(fixture_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    93	    responses = payload.get("endpoints") if isinstance(payload, dict) else None
    94	    if not isinstance(responses, dict):
    95	        raise ValueError(f"fixture must contain an 'endpoints' mapping: {fixture_path}")
    96	
    97	    def query(endpoint: str, **_kwargs: Any):
    98	        rows = responses.get(endpoint)
    99	        if rows is None:
   100	            return pd.DataFrame()
   101	        if isinstance(rows, dict) and "error" in rows:
   102	            raise RuntimeError(rows["error"])
   103	        return pd.DataFrame(rows)
   104	
   105	    return query
   106	
   107	
   108	def load_endpoint_permissions(matrix_path: Path | None) -> tuple[dict[str, str], str | None, str | None]:
   109	    if matrix_path is None or not Path(matrix_path).exists():
   110	        return {}, None, None
   111	    try:
   112	        matrix = json.loads(Path(matrix_path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   113	    except (OSError, ValueError):
   114	        return {}, None, None
   115	    permissions = {
   116	        str(record.get("endpoint")): str(record.get("permission_status"))
   117	        for record in matrix.get("endpoints", [])
   118	        if record.get("endpoint") and record.get("permission_status")
   119	    }
   120	    tier = matrix.get("tier") or "2000_points"
   121	    ref = str(Path(matrix_path).relative_to(ROOT)) if Path(matrix_path).is_relative_to(ROOT) else str(matrix_path)
   122	    return permissions, tier, ref
   123	
   124

   135	        "permission_level": pack["permission_level"],
   136	        "status": pack["status"],
   137	        "summary": pack["summary"],
   138	        "endpoints": {
   139	            endpoint: {
   140	                "state": record["state"],

   391	def _apply_error_result(
   392	    record: dict[str, Any],
   393	    exc: BaseException,
   394	    *,
   395	    token: str,
   396	) -> None:
   397	    state = classify_exception(exc)
   398	    record["state"] = state
   399	    record["error"] = sanitize_error_text(exc, token)
   400	    record["response_sha256"] = payload_sha256(sanitize_error_text(exc, token))
   401	    record["response_sha256_scope"] = "sanitized_error"
   402	    record["row_count"] = None
   403	    record["column_count"] = None
   404	    record["columns"] = None
   405	    record["time_range"] = None
   406	    if state == STATE_RATE_LIMITED:
   407	        record["rate_limit"]["verified_in_run"] = True

   410	def probe_endpoint(
   411	    query_fn: Callable[..., pd.DataFrame],
   412	    spec: EndpointSpec,
   413	    probe_context: Mapping[str, str],
   414	    *,
   415	    token: str = "",
   416	    now: Callable[[], datetime] | None = None,
   417	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   418	    """Execute at most one probe (plus at most one recorded retry).
   419	
   420	    Retry policy: (1) date-bound endpoints that return an empty frame on the
   421	    probe date may retry once on the fallback trading date; (2) a transient
   422	    network error may retry once with identical params.  Permission denial
   423	    and rate limiting must never be retried.
   424	    """
   425	
   426	    timestamp = (now or datetime.now)().astimezone().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   427	    params = build_params(spec, probe_context)
   428	    record = _base_record(spec, params, tested_at=timestamp)
   429	    attempt_params = params
   430	    started = datetime.now()
   431	    try:
   432	        frame = query_fn(spec.endpoint, **attempt_params)
   433	    except Exception as exc:
   434	        _apply_error_result(record, exc, token=token)
   435	        record["first_attempt_state"] = record["state"]
   436	        if record["state"] == STATE_QUERY_FAILED and is_transient_network_error(str(exc)):
   437	            retry_params = attempt_params
   438	            retry_reason = "transient_network_error_single_retry"
   439	            record["attempts"] = 2
   440	            record["retry_reason"] = retry_reason
   441	            try:
   442	                frame = query_fn(spec.endpoint, **retry_params)
   443	            except Exception as retry_exc:
   444	                _apply_error_result(record, retry_exc, token=token)
   445	            else:
   446	                _apply_frame_result(record, frame)
   447	        record["latency_ms"] = int((datetime.now() - started).total_seconds() * 1000)
   448	        record["permission_status"] = permission_status_for(record["state"])
   449	        record["empty_vs_error"] = _empty_vs_error(record["state"])
   450	        return record
   451	    _apply_frame_result(record, frame)
   452	    record["first_attempt_state"] = record["state"]
   453	    if spec.date_bound and record["state"] == STATE_NORMAL_NO_DATA:
   454	        fallback = probe_context.get("fallback_date", "")
   455	        if fallback:
   456	            record["attempts"] = 2
   457	            record["retry_reason"] = "probe_date_no_rows_retry_on_fallback_trading_date"
   458	            try:
   459	                frame = query_fn(
   460	                    spec.endpoint,
   461	                    **with_fallback_date(attempt_params, fallback),
   462	                )
   463	            except Exception as retry_exc:
   464	                _apply_error_result(record, retry_exc, token=token)
   465	            else:
   466	                _apply_frame_result(record, frame)
   467	    record["latency_ms"] = int((datetime.now() - started).total_seconds() * 1000)
   468	    record["permission_status"] = permission_status_for(record["state"])
   469	    record["empty_vs_error"] = _empty_vs_error(record["state"])
   470	    return record
   471	
   472	
   473	def not_queried_record(
   474	    spec: EndpointSpec,
   475	    probe_context: Mapping[str, str],
   476	    reason: str,
   477	    *,
   478	    now: Callable[[], datetime] | None = None,
   479	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   480	    timestamp = (now or datetime.now)().astimezone().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   481	    record = _base_record(spec, build_params(spec, probe_context), tested_at=timestamp)
   482	    record["state"] = STATE_NOT_QUERIED
   483	    record["first_attempt_state"] = STATE_NOT_QUERIED
   484	    record["permission_status"] = PERMISSION_UNKNOWN
   485	    record["empty_vs_error"] = _empty_vs_error(STATE_NOT_QUERIED)
   486	    record["error"] = sanitize_error_text(reason)
   487	    return record

   638	        )
   639	        written, leaks = audit.write_outputs(
   640	            matrix, tmp_path, token=TOKEN, token_source=TOKEN_SOURCE_DOTENV, env_override_applied=True
   641	        )
   642	        assert leaks == []
   643	        assert [path.name for path in written] == [
   644	            "tushare_permission_matrix.json",
   645	            "tushare_permission_matrix.md",
   646	            "run-receipt.md",
   647	        ]
   648	        receipt = (tmp_path / "run-receipt.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
   649	        assert "Token 来源" in receipt and "dotenv_file" in receipt
   650	        assert "env_override_applied=true" in receipt
   651	        assert "repurchase 单股查询记录" in receipt
   652	        matrix_json = json.loads((tmp_path / "tushare_permission_matrix.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   653	        repurchase = next(r for r in matrix_json["endpoints"] if r["endpoint"] == "repurchase")
   654	        assert repurchase["params"] == {"ts_code": "603629.SH"}
   655	        assert TOKEN not in receipt

   657	    def test_main_returns_3_and_writes_nothing_when_probe_leaks_token(self, tmp_path, monkeypatch):

The patch improves the audit flow but still permits probing with an unintended inherited token and can miss serialized credential headers. It also leaves downstream exporters pinned to the superseded matrix.

Full review comments:

- [P1] Reject inherited tokens unless --no-dotenv is explicit — /Users/maybee/TradingAgents-AShare/scripts/audit_tushare_capability.py:100-101
  When an explicitly selected `.env` is missing or lacks `TUSHARE_TOKEN` while the process environment contains one, this branch proceeds with the inherited token even though `--no-dotenv` was not requested. A typo such as `--dotenv wrong.env` can therefore probe the wrong account, contrary to the task's `.env`-only credential contract; inherited fallback should be restricted to the explicit test-isolation path.

- [P1] Detect serialized credential headers before writing artifacts — /Users/maybee/TradingAgents-AShare/scripts/audit_tushare_capability.py:165-169
  If a probe exception contains serialized headers such as `{"Cookie": "short-secret"}`, `sanitize_error_text` leaves that value intact and this scan misses it because the quote appears between `cookie` and `:`. `write_outputs` then reports clean and writes the credential, defeating the new fail-closed guarantee; detect quoted/dict header forms as well as plain `Cookie:` and `Authorization:`.

- [P2] Promote the rerun matrix to downstream defaults — /Users/maybee/TradingAgents-AShare/docs/task_runs/TA-TUSHARE-2000-001A-R1-RERUN-20260816-202237/run-receipt.md:25-25
  For default evidence/governance exports, both exporter scripts still load `TA-TUSHARE-2000-001A-20260816-041709`, so this newly designated truth source is never consumed and generated packs continue recording the old, unauditable `matrix_ref`. Update those defaults or provide a stable pointer to this corrected matrix.
